To address this, we propose Structural Consensus-based KAN Prototype Learning (SCKAN), which constructs the first cross-sample structural consensus learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), to achieve more generalizable and accurate segmentation. Specifically, SCKAN contains two key designs: Structure-constrained Prototype Consistency Learning (SPCL), which prompts unbiased structural representation by enforcing cross-sample consistency via prototype-level contrastive optimization, and Consensus-based Kolmogorov-Arnold Fusion (CKaF), which reduces morphology-specific bias by aggregating stable consensus and filtering sample-wise noise via KAN's adaptive B-spline nonlinearity. Extensive experiments on two public pancreas datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SCKAN. Code is at https://github.com/rhodaliu17/SCKAN.

</details>


### [74] [BEAT: Rhythm-Elastic Alignment for Agentic Music-guided Movie Trailer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27067)
*Yutong Wang,Yunke Wang,Xinyuan Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEAT是一个自动电影预告片生成框架，通过MuVA（基于Sinkhorn正则化的音乐-视觉对齐编码器）和Bar-DP（能量自适应动态规划算法）实现弹性多对一的镜头-音乐对齐，在TrailerArena基准测试中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将音乐对齐降级为后处理步骤，要么强制实行刚性的单镜头-单音乐映射，忽略了专业剪辑中节奏的弹性特征（快速剪辑对应高能量段落，持续镜头对应轻柔小节）。

Method: 提出BEAT框架，包含：(1) MuVA——基于Sinkhorn正则化两阶段学习的紧凑型音乐-视觉对齐编码器；(2) Bar-DP——能量自适应动态规划算法，实现遵循音乐动态的弹性多对一对齐；(3) 五阶段智能体流水线，将核心对齐嵌入跨模态特征，并通过结构化文本信号协调高维度创作决策。

Result: 在TrailerArena基准测试（涵盖20+指标、四个互补维度）上，BEAT在镜头选择、排序和感知质量方面均达到SOTA，并能端到端生成完整预告片。

Conclusion: 弹性多对一的对齐策略优于刚性映射，跨模态特征学习与结构化文本驱动的智能体流水线相结合能有效提升预告片自动生成质量。

Abstract: Automatic movie trailer generation must select shots from a full-length film and synchronize them with background music. Existing methods either relegate music alignment to post-processing or enforce rigid one-to-one shot-music mappings, overlooking that professional editing rhythm is elastic: rapid cuts accompany high-energy passages while sustained shots span quieter bars. We introduce BEAT, a framework that addresses this gap with two core components: MuVA, a compact music-visual alignment encoder trained with Sinkhorn-regularized two-stage learning, and Bar-DP, an energy-adaptive dynamic programming algorithm that produces elastic many-to-one alignments following musical dynamics. These components are integrated into a five-phase agentic pipeline that grounds the core alignment in learned cross-modal features while coordinating higher-level creative decisions through structured text signals. To support comprehensive evaluation, we also introduce TrailerArena, a benchmark with 20+ metrics across four complementary dimensions. On TrailerArena, BEAT achieves state-of-the-art performance across shot selection, ordering, and perceptual quality, while producing fully composed trailers end-to-end.

</details>


### [75] [IPIBench: Evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under Continuous Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27074)
*Jinzhao Li,Yinuo Chen,Wenxuan Song,Yijia Lei,Yichi Zhang,Honglei Yan,Panwang Pan,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IPIBench基准测试，用于评估多模态大语言模型在流式视频场景下的交互式主动智能能力，并设计了IPI-Agent框架来提升模型在主动触发和多轮交互协调方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要研究单轮设置下的被动或主动交互，忽略了流式视频场景中用户可能添加、修改或取消主动请求并与被动查询交织的动态多轮交互场景，因此需要提出新的基准来评估MLLMs的交互式主动智能。

Method: 提出IPIBench基准，涵盖主动监控、主动任务管理和交织的被动-主动请求三类场景；并提出IPI-Agent，一种无需训练的智能体框架，包含交互控制策略和时间门控机制，用于稳定主动触发和协调多轮交互。

Result: 代表性MLLMs在IPIBench上表现出两个主要局限：不稳定的主动触发和被动与主动行为之间的弱协调。IPI-Agent在所有基准设置上一致提升了现有MLLMs的性能。

Conclusion: IPIBench有效揭示现有MLLMs在交互式主动智能方面的不足，IPI-Agent框架无需额外训练即可显著改善模型的主动触发稳定性和多轮交互协调能力。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on reactive question answering, but real-world streaming assistants require proactive reasoning over continuous visual inputs. Existing benchmarks mainly study reactive or proactive interactions in isolated single-turn settings, overlooking dynamic multi-turn scenarios where users may add, modify, or cancel proactive requests alongside interleaved reactive queries. To address this gap, we introduce IPIBench, the first benchmark for evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under streaming video settings. IPIBench covers proactive monitoring, proactive task management, and interleaved reactive-proactive requests. Evaluations on representative MLLMs reveal two major limitations: unstable proactive triggering and weak coordination between reactive and proactive behaviors. We further propose IPI-Agent, a training-free agentic framework with an interaction-control policy and a temporal-gating mechanism for stabilizing proactive triggering and coordinating multi-turn interactions. Experiments show that IPI-Agent consistently improves existing MLLMs across all benchmark settings.

</details>


### [76] [SoftCap: Soft-Budget Control for Diffusion Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27075)
*Yuhang Zhang,Junxiang Qiu,Huixia Ben,Zhenhua Tang,Shuo Wang,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftCap是一个用于扩散Transformer（DiTs）的无训练加速控制层，通过轨迹漂移观测器和软预算PI控制器动态决定何时执行完整计算步骤，在保持计算成本的同时提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiTs）的迭代去噪过程需要大量耗时的Transformer计算，现有加速方法（如缓存、预测或验证中间特征）通常依赖固定调度或手动调整阈值来决定何时执行完整步骤，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: SoftCap包含两个核心组件：1) 轨迹漂移观测器（Trajectory Drift Observer），通过轻量级隐藏状态统计量估计局部缓存风险；2) 软预算PI控制器（Soft-Budget PI Controller），根据已消耗计算量相对于固定参考配置调整触发完整计算的阈值。预算是软上限，仅影响阈值而不强制要求特定数量的完整评估。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上，SoftCap在相近计算量下优于SpeCa，将ImageReward从0.967提升至0.981，LPIPS-Full从0.518降低至0.498。目标扫描诊断显示，随着预算放宽，SoftCap呈现预期的软上限行为。

Conclusion: SoftCap通过自适应的软预算控制机制，在无训练条件下实现了扩散Transformer推理速度与生成质量的更好权衡。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve strong visual quality, but their iterative denoising process requires many costly Transformer evaluations. Training-free acceleration methods reduce this cost by caching, forecasting, or verifying intermediate features, yet the runtime decision of when to execute a Full step is often driven by fixed schedules or hand-tuned thresholds. We propose \textbf{SoftCap}, a training-free control layer for cache-based DiT inference. SoftCap couples a Trajectory Drift Observer, which estimates local cache risk from lightweight hidden-state statistics, with a Soft-Budget PI Controller, which adjusts the Full-triggering threshold from realized compute relative to a fixed reference profile. The budget is a soft ceiling: it shapes the threshold but does not require a run to spend a prescribed number of Full evaluations. On FLUX.1-dev, SoftCap improves over SpeCa at a comparable middle-compute operating point, raising ImageReward from 0.967 to 0.981 and reducing LPIPS-Full from 0.518 to 0.498 at nearly identical FLOPs, while target-sweep diagnostics show the intended soft-ceiling behavior as the budget is relaxed.

</details>


### [77] [Semi-Supervised Gaze Estimation via Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27080)
*Qida Tan,Hongyu Yang,Wenchao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半监督学习的解耦子空间对比学习（DSCL）框架，通过雅可比正则化将特征表示解耦为俯仰角和偏航角等特定注视分量的判别性子空间，并结合子空间内的有序对比学习，仅用5%-20%的标注数据即可在域内和跨域评估中达到竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于外观的注视估计因标注样本有限和数据集多样性不足而泛化能力差，现有方法依赖大规模伪标签数据缓解域偏移，但仍需要大量人工标注。本文旨在通过半监督学习利用未标注数据增强域泛化，减少对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 提出DS（Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning）框架：1）通过雅可比正则化将特征表示解耦为对应不同注视分量（如俯仰角、偏航角）的判别性子空间；2）在每个子空间内利用固有的有序排名进行对比学习，使模型能从少量标注样本和大量未标注样本中学习鲁棒的注视表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，DSCL在域内和跨域评估场景下，仅使用20%、10%甚至5%的标注数据即可达到竞争性性能，且即插即用。

Conclusion: DSCL是一种简单而有效的半监督注视估计方法，通过解耦子空间和有序对比学习显著降低了对人工标注的依赖，具有良好的域泛化能力。

Abstract: Appearance-based gaze estimation always suffers from poor generalization due to limited annotated samples and insufficient dataset diversity. Leading approaches adopt weakly supervised learning to generate large-scale pseudo-labeled data from unconstrained real-world scenarios, aiming to mitigate the domain shifts. In this work, we devise a simple yet effective semi-supervised learning architecture that leverages unlabeled data to enhance domain generalization, thereby reducing reliance on labor-intensive manual annotations. Our key insight is to impose Jacobian regularization to disentangle feature representations into discriminative subspaces dedicated to specific gaze components, such as pitch and yaw angles. We further exploit the intrinsic ordinal ranking within each subspace for contrastive learning, enabling the model to learn robust gaze representations from a small set of labeled samples and an abundance of unlabeled ones. This ultimately yields our Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning (DSCL) framework. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks verify that the proposed DSCL is plug-and-play, achieving competitive performance using only 20\%, 10\%, and even 5\% of the annotated data under both in-domain and cross-domain evaluation settings. The public code is available at \href{https://github.com/da60266/DSCL}{https://github.com/da60266/DSCL}.

</details>


### [78] [Pop-Up Distractions Reveal Bag-of-Events Behavior in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27101)
*Oscar Chew,Serhii Honcharenko,Qian-Hui Chen,Patricia Lu,Dishant Zaveri,Khoa D. Doan,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DistractionBench 来评估 VideoLLMs 在无关视频片段干扰下将主体与事件正确关联的能力，发现所有主流模型均存在严重的"事件袋"幻觉行为，即将不同片段中的实体与事件错误关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VideoLLMs 能否在时间维度上鲁棒地将主体与事件关联尚不明确，作者希望揭示模型在处理包含无关干扰片段的视频时是否存在系统性的幻觉问题。

Method: 设计 DistractionBench 基准，通过在长视频中插入短广告片段作为受控干扰，评估 11 种主流 VideoLLMs 在主体-事件关联上的表现，并定义"事件袋（BoE）"行为来量化模型将视频视为无时序的事件集合而非结构化序列的系统性错误。

Result: 所有 11 种 VideoLLMs 均表现出显著的事件袋行为，频繁将插入广告中的动作错误归因于主视频中的主体，产生跨片段的主体-事件幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前 VideoLLMs 缺乏可靠的时间接地机制，无法在干扰存在时正确关联主体与事件，亟需开发更具鲁棒性的主体-事件关联模型。

Abstract: A key capability for video understanding is reliably linking subjects to events across time, yet whether Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) actually achieve this remains unclear. In this work, we introduce DistractionBench to evaluate whether VideoLLMs can robustly link subjects and events in the presence of unrelated video segments. Through controlled interventions, such as inserting short advertisement clips into longer videos, we show that VideoLLMs frequently hallucinate interactions between entities from different segments, incorrectly attributing actions from injected advertisements to subjects in the main video. We characterize this systematic hallucination as bag-of-events (BoE) behavior, where models process videos as collections of events rather than temporally structured sequences. Evaluating 11 popular VideoLLMs, we find that all models exhibit substantial BoE behavior. Our findings suggest that VideoLLMs lack reliable mechanisms for temporal grounding and motivate the development of models with more robust subject-event association.

</details>


### [79] [COVD: Continual Open-Vocabulary Object Detection with Novel Concept Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27116)
*Yupeng Zhang,Ruize Han,Yuzhong Feng,Zixin Ren,Yuntong Tian,Liang Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出持续开放词汇检测（COVD）任务及NoIn-Det框架，通过冻结视觉编码器、仅更新少量文本分支参数的方式，高效注入新概念同时保持旧知识，无需额外参数，在Novel-114基准上超越现有持续学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中类别空间不断演化，现有OVD模型无法处理新兴概念，而完全重训练代价高昂，因此需要一种能够持续学习新概念同时保持原有开放词汇能力的检测框架。

Method: NoIn-Det框架：冻结视觉编码器，使用常见概念和已注入概念的文本保留文本表示空间，仅更新对新颖概念学习有益的少量文本分支参数来注入新概念，无需额外参数。

Result: NoIn-Det有效学习新概念、保持旧知识，持续优于现有VLMs持续学习方法，且不引入额外参数。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉编码器已能感知许多新概念，主要瓶颈在于视觉表示与文本概念之间缺乏稳定的语义对齐，NoIn-Det通过高效的文本分支微调解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) has made significant progress, enabling detectors to generalize from seen to unseen categories. However, real-world category spaces continually evolve, and existing OVD models still struggle with newly emerging concepts, while repeated full retraining is prohibitively expensive. To this end, we introduce a new task setting, termed Continual OVD with Novel Concept Injection (COVD), where models sequentially learn incoming novel concept groups while preserving prior concepts and original open-vocabulary knowledge, along with a new benchmark, Novel-114. Our key observation is that pretrained visual encoders often already perceive and represent many novel concepts, and the main bottleneck lies in the lack of stable semantic alignment between visual representations and textual concepts. Based on this, we propose NoIn-Det, an efficient continual injection framework without additional parameters. NoIn-Det freezes the visual encoder, preserves the text representation space using only texts of common concepts and previously injected concepts, and injects novel concepts by updating only a small subset of text-branch parameters beneficial to novel concept learning. Extensive experiments show that NoIn-Det effectively learns novel concepts, preserves old knowledge, and consistently outperforms existing continual learning methods for VLMs without introducing additional parameters.Novel-114 and the code will be released.

</details>


### [80] [Image Thresholding: Understanding Bias of Evaluation Metrics towards Specific Evaluation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27132)
*Eslam Hegazy,Mohamed Gabr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了多级图像阈值分割中目标函数（Otsu和Kapur）与质量评估指标（SSIM、PSNR）之间的相关性，发现Otsu准则与两种指标高度相关，而Kapur熵相关性较弱且不稳定，揭示了评估体系中存在固有的度量-目标函数偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在使用SSIM和PSNR评估阈值分割质量时，隐含假设这些指标能无偏地衡量分割效果，但这一假设未经验证。本研究旨在检验该假设的合理性。

Method: 在BSDS500数据集上，对所有可能阈值计算Otsu类间方差和Kapur熵与SSIM、PSNR之间的相关性，进行系统性定量分析。

Result: Otsu准则与SSIM和PSNR均保持高度一致性相关；Kapur熵与二者的相关性较弱且波动较大；Otsu在PSNR相关性上100%优于Kapur，在SSIM相关性上超过91%的图像表现更优。

Conclusion: 阈值分割评估存在固有的度量-目标函数偏差，需建立更中立的评估框架，并建议将分析扩展到更多准则和应用领域。

Abstract: Multilevel image thresholding is widely used for segmentation in applications ranging from medical imaging to remote sensing. Classical objective functions, such as Otsu's between-class variance and Kapur's entropy, are often optimized using metaheuristic algorithms, with performance evaluated via metrics like Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) and Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR). These evaluations implicitly assume that SSIM and PSNR provide unbiased measures of segmentation quality. In this study, we examine this assumption by analyzing the correlation between thresholding objective functions and quality metrics across all possible thresholds for images in the BSDS500 dataset. Results show that Otsu's criterion consistently exhibits high correlation with both SSIM and PSNR, while Kapur's entropy demonstrates weaker and more variable correlation. Otsu outperforms Kapur in correlation with PSNR for all images and with SSIM for over 91%. Our findings reveal an inherent metric-objective-function bias. This work highlights the need for more neutral evaluation frameworks and motivates extending the analysis to additional thresholding criteria and domains. Source code of this paper can be found at https://w3id.org/met-dp/icpr26-95

</details>


### [81] [Leveraging Visual Signals for Robust Token-Level Uncertainty in Vision-Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27136)
*Joseph Hoche,David Brellmann,Gianni Franchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIG-TUQ，一种将视觉信息显式融入不确定性估计的无训练框架，通过加权语言token不确定性与视觉接地分数，在多种LVLM架构上提升不确定性量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM的不确定性量化方法多从LLM领域迁移，忽略了视觉模态对不确定性的贡献，视觉信息的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过分析视觉特征整合后的隐藏表示，发现高置信度预测更依赖视觉内容。基于此，提出VIG-TUQ框架，利用视觉接地分数加权token级语言不确定性，无需额外训练。

Result: 在多个数据集和不同融合架构（早期融合、晚期融合、原生融合）的LVLM上，VIG-TUQ相比现有token级不确定性方法取得了改进。

Conclusion: 将视觉信息显式纳入不确定性估计能有效提升LVLM的UQ性能，VIG-TUQ是一种轻量且有效的训练-free方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) remains a critical challenge in Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) for reliable predictions and real-world deployment. However, most existing methods are adapted from the LLM literature and primarily focus on the language modality, leaving the contribution of visual information to LVLM uncertainty largely underexplored. In this paper, we investigate how LVLMs process visual information and whether this process can be used to improve uncertainty estimation. By analyzing hidden representations after the integration of visual features during the generation process, we observe that high-confidence predictions rely more heavily on visual content than uncertain ones. Building on this insight, we propose Visual-Grounded Token UQ (VIG-TUQ), a training-free framework that explicitly incorporates visual grounding into uncertainty estimation by weighting token-level language uncertainty with visual grounding scores. We evaluate VIG-TUQ on multiple datasets and across diverse LVLM architectures, including early-fusion, late-fusion, and native-fusion models. Results indicate that our method often improves upon existing token-level uncertainty approaches. Code and data will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [82] [Is an Image Also Worth 16x16=256 Superpixels? A Framework for Attentional Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27144)
*Pedro Henrique da Costa Avelar,Anderson R. Tavares,Luís C. Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Superpixel Transformers (SPT)框架，统一了超像素图像分类与Vision Transformers，通过引入多维正弦-余弦位置编码和增强补丁数据结构，在多个数据集上超越了现有超像素GNN方法并与ViT性能相当，展示了超像素与Transformer模型的协同潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超像素图像分类主要依赖GNN，而ViT在视觉任务中表现优异，但GNN、超像素与Transformer三者之间缺乏有效的协同连接。SPT旨在弥合这一鸿沟，探索超像素与Transformer混合框架的潜力。

Method: SPT通过泛化SICGAT模型和ViT，支持任意超像素切分策略、连通图结构和位置编码。引入多维正弦-余弦位置编码和增强补丁数据结构，完整编码超像素的形状和颜色信息，通过约束图连通性增强ViT性能。

Result: 在CIFAR10、FashionMNIST和Imagenette数据集上，SPT优于现有超像素GNN方法，与ViT性能相当。有效解决了SICGAT中像素聚合信息损失的问题。

Conclusion: SPT成功桥接了超像素模型与Transformer模型之间的差距，为跨域泛化和混合注意力框架的未来创新开辟了新途径，证明图像也可以通过超像素形式进行有效处理。

Abstract: Superpixel-based image classification has traditionally leveraged graph neural networks (GNNs) for processing irregular image representations. Recent advances in computer vision, driven by Vision Transformers (ViTs), have introduced new paradigms in self-attentional models, surpassing convolutional neural networks (CNNs) in various tasks. However, a synergistic connection between GNNs, superpixels, and transformers remains unexplored. In this work, we propose Superpixel Transformers (SPT), a novel framework that unifies superpixel-based image classification and ViTs. SPT generalizes the Superpixel Image Classification with Graph Attention Networks (SICGAT) model and ViT to support arbitrary superpixel-based chunking strategies, connectivity graphs, and positional encodings. We introduce refinements including a multidimensional sine-cosine positional encoding and an enriched patch data structure that fully incorporates superpixel shape and color information. By testing SPT across datasets such as CIFAR10, FashionMNIST, and Imagenette, with various superpixel generation and graph connectivity strategies, we demonstrate that SPT achieves superior performance compared to previous superpixel-based GNN methods and remains competitive with ViTs. Notably, our approach addresses the limitations of SICGAT, such as information loss during pixel aggregation, and shows how constrained graph connectivity can enhance ViT performance. SPT bridges the gap between superpixel-based and transformer models, opening avenues for cross-domain generalization and future innovations in hybrid attentional frameworks, and showing that an image can also be worth $16\times16$ superpixels.

</details>


### [83] [Touch-R1: Reinforcing Touch Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27154)
*Yingxin Lai,Yafei Zhou,Fucai Zhu,Siyu Zhu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Touch-R1，一种基于GRPO强化学习的触觉推理多模态大模型，在触觉推理基准上超越Octopi-13B和GPT-4o，并展现出探索、比较和修正等涌现推理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉-语言模型主要依赖监督或对比学习目标，无法将预测基于物理证据，也难以纠正误导性的视觉先验；触觉推理面临物理属性序数性和跨传感器分布偏移两大特有挑战。

Method: 构建TouchReason-1M（含100万+四类传感器同步触觉对）和TouchReason-Bench评估框架；基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B提出Touch-R1，采用触觉驱动的GRPO目标，结合序数感知准确性、跨传感器物理一致性、结构化格式控制及输入侧触觉接地目标，其中触觉使用奖励仅在真实触觉输入优于反事实控制（去除/打乱/加噪触觉流）时赋予奖励。

Result: Touch-R1-7B在TouchReason-Bench上平均超越Octopi-13B 18.4%、GPT-4o 24.7%。

Conclusion: R1风格的强化学习推理可以有效扎根于物理触觉接触，模型展现出探索、比较与修正等涌现行为。

Abstract: While rule-based reinforcement learning has recently catalyzed explicit reasoning in multimodal models, tactile reasoning remains largely underexplored. Existing tactile-language models primarily rely on supervised or contrastive objectives, which limits their capacity to ground predictions in physical evidence or rectify misleading visual priors. Tactile reasoning introduces two modality-specific challenges: the ordinal nature of physical attributes (e.g., hardness, roughness) and the cross-sensor distribution shifts inherent in optical tactile hardware. In this work, we introduce TouchReason-1M, a large-scale multimodal dataset comprising over 1M synchronized tactile pairs across four distinct sensors, and TouchReason-Bench, a rigorous framework for evaluating tactile perception and visual-tactile conflict resolution. Building upon these, we propose Touch-R1, a tactile reasoning MLLM based on Qwen2.5-VL-7B. Touch-R1 is trained via a tactile-grounded GRPO objective that combines ordinal-aware accuracy, cross-sensor physical consistency, structured-format control, and an input-side tactile grounding objective. Specifically, the tactile-use reward assigns credit only when authentic tactile inputs yield superior correctness relative to counterfactual controls where the tactile stream is removed, shuffled, or noise-masked. On TouchReason-Bench, Touch-R1-7B outperforms Octopi-13B by 18.4\% and GPT-4o by 24.7\% on average. Its structured reasoning traces reveal emergent behaviors of probing, comparison, and revision, demonstrating that R1-style reasoning can be effectively grounded in physical contact.

</details>


### [84] [Semantic Robustness Probing via Inpainting: An Interactive Tool for Safety-Critical Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27155)
*Nico Steckhan,Krutarth Prajapati,Weija Shao,Silvia Vock*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemProbe是一个用于语义鲁棒性探测的工具，通过扩散模型引导的图像修补技术生成语义有意义的测试样本，帮助评估安全关键领域中的目标检测器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键领域（如工业设备）中测试目标检测器需要超越像素级损坏的语义上有意义的探测方法，现有方法缺乏对语义因素的灵活可控生成能力。

Method: 用户上传部署图像，手动或自动创建掩码，选择源自运行设计域或自定义提示的因素，利用扩散模型进行受控图像修补。支持批量作业、并行种子/工作流变体及可配置生成参数，输出后自动执行模型推理并显示带性能差异的注释前后对比，所有探测记录为结构化工件。

Result: 该系统在手部检测（针对尺寸锯）的应用中得到了验证，目标因素源自保险导向的测试标准，展示了生成语义鲁棒性探测的能力。

Conclusion: SemProbe能够生成可追溯的鲁棒性证据，与安全评估工作流对齐，为安全关键领域的检测器评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Testing object detectors in safety-critical domains requires semantically meaningful probes beyond pixel-level corruptions. We present SemProbe, a tool for semantic robustness probing: users upload deployment images, create masks manually or automatically, select operational design domain-derived factors (or custom prompts), and run diffusion-based controlled inpainting. The system supports batch jobs, parallel seed/workflow variations, and configurable generation parameters. After each output, model inference runs automatically and displays annotated before/after comparisons with performance deltas. All probes are logged as structured artifacts, enabling traceable robustness evidence aligned with safety evaluation workflows. We demonstrate \textsc{SemProbe} on hand detection for dimension saws, targeting factors from insurance-oriented test criteria.

</details>


### [85] [Generative Animations: A Multi-Model Pipeline for Prompt-Driven Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27203)
*Mannat Khurana,Sanyam Jain,Rishav Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Generative Animations系统，通过链式调用LLM进行语义解析与SAM进行视觉定位，将自然语言提示自动转化为符合场景几何、深度遮挡和3D透视的生产级动画路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动画创作中，自定义运动路径需要设计师手动选择预设、绘制贝塞尔曲线和配置时间属性，过程繁琐且效率低下，缺乏从自然语言到动画的自动化转化方案。

Method: 采用流水线架构：先通过大语言模型（LLM）对自然语言提示进行语义解析，再结合Segment Anything Model（SAM）进行视觉定位，自动生成遵循场景几何约束、处理深度遮挡并适配3D透视变换的运动路径。

Result: 在三个用例中验证：轮廓跟随轨迹、带Z轴顺序感知的轨道动画、以及变换对象上的透视对齐运动。

Conclusion: 该系统将自然语言提示自动化转化为生产级动画，显著降低了运动路径设计的手动操作成本，实现了语义理解与视觉几何的端到端融合。

Abstract: Animation elevates digital documents into immersive experiences, yet creating custom motion paths remains cumbersome, requiring designers to manually select presets, plot Bézier points, and configure timing properties. We introduce Generative Animations, a system that transforms natural language prompts into production-ready animations. By chaining Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic parsing with the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for visual grounding, our pipeline automatically generates motion paths that respect scene geometry, handle depth-based occlusions, and honor 3D perspective transforms. We demonstrate the system through three use cases: contour-following trajectories, orbital animations with z-order awareness, and perspective-aligned motion on transformed objects.

</details>


### [86] [MRT: Masked Region Transformer for Layered Image Generation and Editing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27235)
*Zhicong Tang,Zhao Zhang,Jingye Chen,Mohan Zhou,Yifan Pu,Yuchi Liu,Yalong Bai,Ethan Smith,Yuhui Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MRT是一个200亿参数的掩码区域扩散模型，用于多层透明图像生成与编辑，在三个互补任务（文本到图层、图像到图层、图层到图层）上显著超越现有方法，包括Qwen-Image-Layered等商业系统，并实现10-100倍更快的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层图像生成与编辑是实现视觉内容图层级复用、编辑和组合的基础能力，类似于自然语言中的单词级编辑，但该领域在大规模场景下仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MRT模型，基于掩码区域扩散框架统一三个互补任务（文本到图层、图像到图层、图层到图层），通过选择性token掩码实现灵活的图层生成与编辑；引入溢出感知画布层处理边界不一致性并支持半透明背景合成；应用扩散蒸馏实现8步实时生成。

Result: 在三个任务上均显著超越现有最先进方法（包括多个商业系统）；在图像到图层任务上，用户研究结果表明质量优于Qwen-Image-Layered模型，同时推理速度提升10-100倍，激活GPU内存消耗降低50-90%。

Conclusion: MRT为多层透明图像生成建立了新的基准，证明了大规模掩码区域扩散模型在该任务上的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Layered image generation and editing is a fundamental capability that enables layer-wise reuse, editing, and composition of generated visual content, analogous to word-level editing in natural language. Despite its importance, this remains an underexplored area at scale. To address this gap, we present MRT, a 20B-parameter masked region diffusion model tailored for multi-layer transparent image generation and editing, trained on over 10M multilingual design samples spanning diverse aspect ratios and textual prompts. To fully leverage this scale, we make two key technical contributions. First, we unify three complementary tasks including text-to-layers, image-to-layers, and layers-to-layers within a shared masked region diffusion framework, where selective token masking enables flexible layer-wise generation and editing. Second, to enable overflow layer generation, we introduce an overflow-aware canvas layer that handles boundary inconsistencies and supports semi-transparent background synthesis, enabling complete editable layers extending beyond visible canvas boundaries. Additionally, we apply diffusion distillation to achieve 8-step, real-time multi-layer generation with minimal quality degradation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework substantially outperforms prior state-of-the-art approaches, including various commercial systems, across all three tasks, establishing a new benchmark for multi-layer transparent image generation. Notably, our model significantly outperforms the concurrent Qwen-Image-Layered model in image-to-layers quality according to user-study results, while achieving 10-100\times faster inference and reducing activation GPU memory consumption by 50-90\% during image-to-layer inference.

</details>


### [87] [Can Retrieval Heads See Images? Multimodal Retrieval Heads in Long-Context Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27243)
*Aaron Branson Cigres Li,Zhaowei Wang,Yu Zhao,Yiming Du,Haobo Li,Xiyu Ren,Ginny Wong,Simon See,Lishu Luo,Haodong Duan,Pasquale Minervini,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态检索头检测方法，发现多模态大语言模型中仅4.4-10.2%的注意力头贡献了50%的正检索分数，这些头稀疏、内在且因果重要；掩码顶部5%的头会导致性能大幅下降（如MMLongBench-Doc从48.2%降至5.7%），而无需训练即可直接用于视觉丰富文档的排序提升7.7个Recall@1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的检索头研究基于复制标准，无法直接适用于图像证据；多模态大模型需在交错文本和图像中定位相关证据，但缺乏有效的多模态检索头检测方法。

Method: 提出多模态检索头检测方法，从问题词元到文本或视觉证据的注意力分数进行评分；通过掩码实验验证因果重要性，并分析跨模态共享性与模态内动态性。

Result: 多模态检索头仅占4.4-10.2%；掩码顶部5%头使MMLongBench-Doc从48.2%降至5.7%，SlideVQA从71.2%降至8.9%；Qwen3-VL-8B选头评分在MMDocIR上页面检索Recall@1提升7.7/7.4宏/微平均，布局检索提升6.3/6.8点。

Conclusion: 多模态检索头稀疏、内在且因果重要，部分跨模态共享但模态内动态变化；无需训练可直接用于文档排序，显著超越现有基线。

Abstract: Large vision-language models increasingly rely on long-context modeling to reason over documents, hour-level videos, and long-horizon agent trajectories, requiring them to locate relevant evidence across interleaved text and images. Prior work has studied this behavior using retrieval heads in large language models, but its copy-based criterion does not directly apply when evidence appears in images. We introduce a multimodal retrieval head detection method that scores attention from question tokens to textual or visual evidence. With this method, we show that multimodal retrieval heads are sparse, intrinsic, and causally important: only 4.4-10.2% of attention heads account for 50% of the positive retrieval-score mass, and masking the top-5% selected heads drops MMLongBench-Doc from 48.2% to 5.7% and SlideVQA from 71.2% to 8.9%, while random-head masking is far less damaging. Further analysis shows that these heads are partly shared across modalities yet remain dynamic within each modality, with image retrieval heads changing more than text retrieval heads as context length and haystack modality change. Without further training, we find that these heads can also be used directly to rank visually rich documents: on MMDocIR, Qwen3-VL-8B selected-head scoring improves Recall@1 by 7.7/7.4 macro/micro points for page retrieval and 6.3/6.8 points for layout retrieval over the strongest reported baseline.

</details>


### [88] [A Dynamic Programming Framework for Discovering Count and Values of Multilevel Image Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27287)
*Eslam Hegazy,Mohamed Gabr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于动态规划（DP）和改进的最小误差阈值法（MET）的多级图像阈值分割方法（MET-DP），能够自动确定合适的阈值数量，在阈值数量较大时相比传统DP方法速度显著提升，但在SSIM和PSNR指标上不如需要人工输入阈值数量的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多级图像阈值分割方法需要用户手动输入期望的阈值数量，缺乏自动确定合适阈值数量的能力。因此，提出一种能够从输入图像中自动确定合适阈值数量的阈值分割方法具有实际优势。

Method: 基于动态规划算法（DP）与改进的最小误差阈值法（MET）相结合，提出MET-DP方法。通过经验统计研究分析该方法优越性的原因，并在自然、卫星和医学图像数据集上与多种现有方法进行对比。

Result: MET-DP方法在阈值数量较高时，运行时间远小于传统动态规划阈值方法；能够自动为大多数不同类型测试图像检测到合适的阈值数量。但传统需要人工输入阈值数量的方法在SSIM和PSNR指标上优于MET-DP。

Conclusion: MET-DP方法在自动确定阈值数量和计算效率（尤其是高阈值数场景）方面具有优势，但在分割质量（SSIM、PSNR）上仍有提升空间，传统方法在这些指标上表现更好。

Abstract: Multilevel Image thresholding is an important preprocessing algorithm in computer vision applications nowadays. Since most common thresholding methods take the desired count of thresholds as input by the user, thresholding methods that automatically determines a suitable count of thresholds from the input image itself are advantageous. In this article, a novel thresholding method based on a dynamic programming algorithm and a modification of Minimum Error Thresholding (MET) criterion is thoroughly presented. An empirical statistical study is performed to pinpoint why this proposed method is superior. Moreover, an extended comparison between this proposed method and other state-of-the-art methods is performed on a comprehensive set of natural, satellite and medical test images. The numerical results show that the proposed MET-DP method takes much less time than traditional dynamic programming thresholding methods when the number of thresholds is high. The proposed method can detect a suitable count of thresholds for most of tested images of different types. However, traditional methods that take the count of thresholds as input produce thresholded images of higher structural similarity index measure (SSIM) and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) values than MET-DP. Source code can be found on https://w3id.org/met-dp/article1-code

</details>


### [89] [Gemini Embedding 2: A Native Multimodal Embedding Model from Gemini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27295)
*Madhuri Shanbhogue,Zhe Li,Shanfeng Zhang,Gustavo Hernández Ábrego,Shih-Cheng Huang,Aashi Jain,Daniel Salz,Sonam Goenka,Chaitra Hegde,Ji Ma,Feiyang Chen,Jiaxing Wu,Tanmaya Dabral,Babak Samari,Kevin Poulet,Daniel Cer,Kaifeng Chen,Paul Suganathan,Hui Hui,Jovan Andonov,Philippe Schlattner,Jay Han,Iftekhar Naim,Wing Lowe,Vladimir Pchelin,Albert Yang,Yi-Ting Chen,Zhongli Ding,Grace Zhang,Georg Heigold,Yichang Chen,Antoine Reveillon,Brendan Mccloskey,Wenlei Zhou,Dahun Kim,Rui Meng,Emma Wang,Jack Zheng,Halley Fede,Zhen Yang,Keegan Mosley,Brian Potetz,Sahil Dua,Henrique Schechter Vera,Shen Gao,Hesen Zhang,Andreas Hess,Hengxuan Ying,Alberto Montes,Karan Gill,Min Choi,Sebastian Russo,Anja Hauth,Jinhyuk Lee,Michael Boratko,Megan Barnes,Vikram Rao,Claudiu Musat,Cyril Allauzen,Ehsan Variani,Shankar Kumar,Tom Bagby,Junyi Jiao,Yang Gu,Tengxin Li,Ayush Agrawal,Roberto Santana,Dev Nath,Stephen Karukas,Shuoxuan Han,Lucia Loher,Alice Twu,Nidhi Vyas,Siddharth Bhai,Frank Palma Gomez,Wangyuan Zhang,Chaoren Liu,Jizheng Yang,Steve Qiu,Shijie Zhang,Sujay Kulkarni,Sascha Rothe,Sean Nakamoto,Raphael Hoffmann,Zach Gleicher,Yunhsuan Sung,Qin Yin,Tom Duerig,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gemini Embedding 2 是一种原生多模态嵌入模型，能够将视频、音频、图像和文本等模态统一到同一表示空间中，通过大规模对比学习和多任务多阶段训练，在单模态、跨模态和多模态检索任务上达到 SOTA 性能，并展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的嵌入模型通常只支持单模态或有限的多模态组合，缺乏能够在统一表示空间中对视频、音频、图像和文本等多种模态进行联合嵌入的通用模型，难以满足跨模态检索、RAG 等下游任务的多样化需求。

Method: 基于 Gemini 的多模态能力，采用大规模对比学习，在多任务多阶段训练框架下，将视频、音频、图像和文本等模态的任意交错输入映射到统一的表示空间中。

Result: 在多个基准上取得 SOTA 结果：MSCOCO 上 R@1 达 62.9，Vatex 上 NDCG@10 达 68.8，MTEB 多语言任务达 69.9，MTEB 代码任务达 84.0，超越了专门的单模态模型。

Conclusion: Gemini Embedding 2 作为统一多模态嵌入模型，在多种检索任务上超越专门模型，且零样本性能稳健，适用于 RAG、推荐、搜索等下游场景，甚至能泛化到天文、生物科学、美术和烹饪等专业领域。

Abstract: We introduce Gemini Embedding 2, a native multimodal embedding model that allows embedding video, audio, image, and text modalities in a unified representation space. We leverage the multimodal capabilities of Gemini to produce embeddings for arbitrary combinations of interleaved inputs across all these modalities that generalize well across a wide variety of tasks. Applying large-scale contrastive learning in a multi-task multi-stage training setup, we achieve state-of-the-art performance on key embedding benchmarks including unimodal, cross-modal, and multimodal retrieval spanning a diverse set of tasks. We show that our embedding model demonstrates strong performance (with a score of 62.9 R@1 on MSCOCO, 68.8 NDCG@10 on Vatex, 69.9 on MTEB multilingual and 84.0 on MTEB Code) across a variety of tasks surpassing the performance of specialized models. These unified capabilities make Gemini Embedding 2 a promising candidate for downstream use cases such as RAG, recommendation and search. Furthermore, its robust zero-shot performance across distinct fields - from astronomy and bioscience to fine arts and the culinary arts - establishes it as a highly reliable, out-of-the-box representation even for specialized domains.

</details>


### [90] [How and What to Imagine? Visual Thinking in Unified Multimodal Models for Cross-View Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27310)
*Qian Yang,Ankur Sikarwar,Huy Le,Le Zhang,Zhuan Shi,Perouz Taslakian,Aishwarya Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉语言模型在跨视角空间推理中的弱点，提出View Dropout（VDrop）训练策略和全景视觉思维方法，使统一多模态模型生成中间思维图像并真正利用其进行推理，在多个真实世界基准上实现最优跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在跨视角空间推理中表现薄弱，因为它们倾向于用语言推理而丢失细粒度几何信息。虽然"用图像思考"的概念被提出，但模型往往会忽略中间思维图像中的视觉证据，未能真正利用这些视觉线索进行推理。

Method: 提出View Dropout（VDrop）训练策略，在训练时隐藏输入视图的部分区域使其对答案预测不可见，同时对思维图像保持可见，强制模型依赖思维图像进行回答。在此基础上，比较三种视觉思维变体（自上而下、全景、点匹配渲染），并框架化为可学习性与信息量之间的权衡。

Result: 在合成场景上训练并在五个真实世界跨域基准上评估，全景视觉思维结合VDrop是唯一既具信息量又可学习的配置，取得了最佳跨域泛化性能。

Conclusion: 使视觉思维真正起效的关键在于通过VDrop强制模型依赖思维图像，而全景渲染作为视觉思维形式在信息量和可学习性之间取得了最佳平衡。

Abstract: Cross-view spatial reasoning remains a weak spot for vision-language models (VLMs): they often reason in language and lose the fine-grained geometry needed for the task. Thinking with images aims to address this by generating an intermediate thinking image, but recent work shows that models often ignore the visual evidence in these traces. We therefore ask how to make visual thinking matter, and what kind of visual thinking works best. We study these questions in unified multimodal models (UMMs), which natively support interleaved image-text generation. For the first question, we propose View Dropout (VDrop), a training-time intervention that hides parts of one input view from the answer span while keeping them visible to the thinking-image tokens. This encourages the model to use the thinking image when answering, instead of relying only on the input views. Once the thinking image is used for answer prediction, we study which type of visual thinking is most effective. We frame this as a learnability-informativeness tradeoff and compare three thinking-image variants: top-down, panoramic, and point-matching renderings. Trained on synthetic scenes and evaluated on five real-world out-of-domain benchmarks, panoramic visual thinking with VDrop is the only configuration that is both informative and learnable, and it achieves the best out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [91] [Q-GeoMem: Question-Guided Geometric Memory for Video Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27318)
*Xianqiang Gao,Qizhi Chen,Delin Qu,Haoming Song,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题引导的几何记忆框架，通过注入相机条件的几何信息、维护细粒度上下文库和语义-几何证据库、以及基于问题相关性和新颖性的评分机制，在视频空间推理任务上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频空间推理模型将记忆视为通用时间缓存，会引入冗余或无关的几何信息，削弱长程推理能力。

Method: 提出question-guided geometric memory框架：将相机条件几何注入视觉token；维护Fine-Grained Context Bank（近期密集特征与相机状态）和Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank（紧凑长程证据）；通过Q-Former计算问题相关性与新颖性的乘积对候选帧评分；基于容量替换规则保持记忆紧凑；推理时先读取再更新记忆，并与当前帧表示自适应融合。

Result: 在VSI-Bench和VSTI-Bench上达到SOTA性能，消融实验验证了证据评分机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 问题引导的几何记忆框架能有效筛选关键几何证据，提升视频空间推理的长程准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Video spatial reasoning requires accumulating viewpoint-dependent evidence over time while retaining information useful to the question being asked. Existing spatial video-language models improve geometric perception and long-range context modeling, but often treat memory as a generic temporal cache, which can introduce redundant or irrelevant geometry and weaken long-horizon reasoning. We propose \textbf{\ours}, a question-guided geometric memory framework for video spatial reasoning. \ours injects camera-conditioned geometry into visual tokens and maintains two complementary memories: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent dense features and camera states, and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for compact long-range evidence. Each candidate frame is scored by the product of Q-Former-based question relevance and novelty with respect to the retained bank; this score is stored and reused during reading, while a capacity-based replacement rule keeps the bank compact. During reasoning, both memories are read before update and adaptively fused with the current frame representation. Experiments on VSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench show that \ours achieves state-of-the-art performance among evaluated spatial reasoning models, validating the effectiveness of question-guided geometric memory. Ablations further verify the contribution of the proposed evidence scoring mechanism.

</details>


### [92] [Towards Controllable Image Generation through Representation-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27343)
*Nithesh Chandher Karthikeyan,Jonas Unger,Gabriel Eilertsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用预训练自监督模型表示作为扩散模型的条件，旨在减少对标注数据集的依赖，并展现了在生成质量和可控性方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型依赖文本提示或语义图等条件机制，需要大量标注数据集，这限制了模型的实用性和灵活性。

Method: 使用预训练自监督模型的表示作为扩散模型的条件（自条件机制），并在表示空间中识别变化方向以控制生成。

Result: 自条件机制提升了无条件图像生成的质量；表示空间具有平滑性和解缠性，可用于控制生成过程。

Conclusion: 自监督表示可以作为扩散模型的有效条件，减少对标注数据的依赖，并在可控生成方面展现出有前景的性质。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for high-quality image generation and editing, but guiding these models to produce specific outputs remains a challenge. Conventional approaches rely on conditioning mechanisms, such as text prompts or semantic maps, which require extensively annotated datasets. In this preliminary work, we explore diffusion models conditioned on representations from a pre-trained self-supervised model. The self-conditioning mechanism not only improves the quality of unconditional image generation, but also provides a representation space that can be used to control the generation. We explore this conditioning space by identifying directions of variations, and demonstrate promising properties in terms of smoothness and disentanglement.

</details>


### [93] [LocateAnything: Fast and High-Quality Vision-Language Grounding with Parallel Box Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27365)
*Shihao Wang,Shilong Liu,Yuanguo Kuang,Xinyu Wei,Yangzhou Liu,Zhiqi Li,Yunze Man,Guo Chen,Andrew Tao,Guilin Liu,Jan Kautz,Lei Zhang,Zhiding Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LocateAnything提出并行框解码(PBD)方法，将边界框和点作为原子单元单步解码，替代传统的序列化坐标词元生成，结合1.38亿训练样本的大规模数据集，在视觉定位和检测任务中实现了解码吞吐量和定位精度的同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM将视觉定位和检测建模为坐标词元序列生成问题，每个2D框被序列化为多个1D词元独立学习解码，这种逐词元解码方式与框几何结构的耦合特性不匹配，且顺序生成造成推理瓶颈。

Method: 提出并行框解码(PBD)，将边界框和点等几何元素作为原子单元单步解码；构建LocateAnything-Data大规模数据集（超1.38亿训练样本），提升数据多样性。

Result: PBD同时提升了解码吞吐量和定位精度，在速度-精度前沿上取得显著进步，在多个基准上实现了更高的高IoU定位质量。

Conclusion: 并行框解码与大规模训练数据具有互补优势，能够实现高效且精确的统一视觉定位和检测。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) commonly formulate visual grounding and detection as a coordinate-token generation problem, serializing each 2D box into multiple 1D tokens that are learned and decoded largely independently. This token-by-token decoding mismatches the coupled structure of box geometry and creates a practical inference bottleneck due to strictly sequential generation. We introduce LocateAnything, a unified generative grounding and detection framework based on Parallel Box Decoding (PBD). By decoding geometric elements such as bounding boxes and points as atomic units in a single step, LocateAnything preserves intra-box geometric coherence and unlocks substantial parallelism. We show that PBD improves both decoding throughput and localization accuracy. We further develop a scalable data engine and curate LocateAnything-Data, a large-scale dataset with more than 138 million training samples, substantially increasing data diversity for high-precision localization. Extensive evaluations show that LocateAnything advances the speed-accuracy frontier, achieving significantly higher decoding throughput while improving high-IoU localization quality across diverse benchmarks. The results highlight the complementary benefits of Parallel Box Decoding and large-scale training data in enabling efficient and precise unified visual grounding and detection.

</details>


### [94] [SpatialBench: Is Your Spatial Foundation Model an All-Round Player?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27367)
*Haosong Peng,Hao Li,Jiaqi Chen,Yuhao Pan,Runmao Yao,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Haozhao Wang,Dingwen Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialBench基准，系统评估了41个空间基础模型在6种范式、5个空间领域、19个数据集和4种输入密度下的泛化能力，发现当前模型尚未成为全能型玩家，并揭示了全上下文注意力与有界内存策略的权衡，以及领域对齐和数据质量比数据规模更关键。此外，还引入DA-Next-5M数据集和DA-Next基线模型以填补最大数据缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间基础模型评估局限于特定设计领域、有限场景域和任意帧采样，无法全面衡量模型在多样化下游任务、任意视角、场景域变化、输入密度可变及硬件约束下的真实泛化能力，亟需一个跨范式、多领域的统一评估基准。

Method: 构建SpatialBench基准，包含19个数据集、546个场景、5个空间域，采用确定性采样设计；系统评估41个模型（6种范式）在5个任务套件和4种输入密度设置下的表现；分析全上下文注意力与有界内存策略的性能-可扩展性权衡；提出DA-Next-5M大规模数据集和DA-Next基线模型。

Result: 当前模型尚未成为全能型玩家；全上下文注意力最大化精度，有界内存策略实现长序列可扩展性；在具身和以自我为中心的任务中，严格的领域对齐和高数据质量比简单数据扩缩更重要；最大数据缺口在于具身/以自我为中心领域的大规模高质量数据。

Conclusion: SpatialBench揭示了现有空间基础模型泛化能力的局限性，强调了领域对齐和数据质量的关键作用，并提供了DA-Next-5M/DA-Next以推动空间表示学习的边界。

Abstract: While spatial foundation models have demonstrated impressive performance on standard datasets, a critical question remains: are they truly all-round players capable of generalizing robustly across diverse downstream tasks, arbitrary viewpoints, shifting scene domains, varying input densities, and specific hardware constraints? Answering this overarching question requires a holistic assessment, yet current models are mainly evaluated on specific domains for which they were specifically designed or trained. Such evaluations are intrinsically limited by narrow paradigm coverage, limited scene domains, and arbitrary frame sampling, making it fundamentally difficult to assess their true generalization capabilities. To address this gap, we present SpatialBench, a cross-paradigm, domain-diverse benchmark for spatial foundation models with deterministic sampling. SpatialBench features unprecedented scale and rigorous deterministic design, comprising 19 datasets and 546 scenes across 5 diverse spatial domains. It comprehensively evaluates 41 models across 6 paradigms on 5 task suites under 4 different input density settings. Our extensive evaluation reveals that current models are not yet all-round players, and uncovers crucial insights for future advancement. Specifically, we demonstrate that full-context attention maximizes accuracy while bounded-memory strategies unlock long-sequence scalability. Moreover, our empirical evaluations in challenging embodied and egocentric tasks demonstrate that strict domain alignment and high data quality are far more critical to performance than simple dataset scaling. Furthermore, to address the largest data gap identified in our analysis, we go beyond evaluation by introducing a large-scale dataset, DA-Next-5M, and a strong baseline model, DA-Next, pushing the boundaries of spatial representation learning.

</details>


### [95] [G3T Up! Gravity Aligned Coordinate Frames Simplify Pointmap Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27372)
*Bharath Raj Nagoor Kani,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G3T模型，通过预测重力对齐的点图而非相机中心坐标系点图，利用真实场景中的重力结构先验，提升3D重建精度；并进一步提出G3T-Long子图增量重建管线，利用重力对齐降低旋转自由度，显著改善重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法（如VGGT）预测相机坐标系下的点图，但该坐标系并非最优选择；真实场景中存在强结构先验（重力方向），利用这一先验可以简化多视角点图之间的对齐（减少旋转自由度），从而提升重建精度。

Method: 从现有模型出发，在重力对齐的3D数据上进行微调，得到G3T（重力对齐几何变换器），输出直立点图和相机到重力的位姿；进一步提出G3T-Long，一种基于子图的增量式3D重建管线，利用直立坐标系下减少的旋转自由度实现更优重建。

Result: G3T能够生成高精度的重力感知预测（直立点图及相机-重力位姿）；G3T-Long通过利用重力对齐坐标系的低旋转自由度，显著提升了3D重建的精度。

Conclusion: 将预测坐标系从相机中心坐标系改为重力对齐坐标系，能够有效利用真实场景的结构先验，降低多视图对齐难度，是提升前馈3D重建性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods like VGGT predict pixel-aligned pointmaps in camera-centric coordinate frames. However, this choice of coordinate frame is not always optimal. We propose instead to predict pointmaps in upright, gravity-aligned frames that exploit strong structural cues present in many real-world scenes. Unlike camera-centric frames, gravity-aligned frames share a common vertical axis across viewpoints, reducing the rotational degrees of freedom needed to relate pointmaps to one another. To this end, we introduce the Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer (G3T), fine-tuned from existing models on gravity-aligned 3D data. G3T produces highly accurate gravity-aware predictions, including upright pointmaps and camera-to-gravity poses. We further introduce G3T-Long, a submap-based incremental 3D reconstruction pipeline that leverages the reduced rotational degrees of freedom afforded by upright frames to achieve significantly improved reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Self-Verified Distillation: Your Language Model Is Secretly Its Own Synthetic Data Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26132)
*Tony Lee,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Verified Distillation（自验证蒸馏）方法，让大语言模型仅使用无标注种子问题，通过自生成答案、三级级联验证（循环一致性、事实性、正确性）过滤，再对过滤后的数据进行自训练，从而提升推理能力。在数学、科学、编程三个领域，Qwen3-4B模型分别获得+16.7、+11.1、+8.3个点的pass@1提升，且相比测试时计算开销大的方法，本方法仅需单次推理调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型能否仅依靠无标注提示（无外部教师或工具反馈）进行自我改进，从而降低对人工标注数据和外部监督的依赖。

Method: 提出Self-Verified Distillation算法：模型对无标注种子问题生成候选解，通过基于提示的自验证三级级联过滤（循环一致性检查→事实性检查→正确性检查，需所有评审一致通过），用过滤后的高质量自筛选数据集进行训练。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，数学领域（AIME26和HMMT）pass@1提升+16.7点，科学领域（GPQA Diamond和HLE）提升+11.1点，编程领域（LCBv5和LCBv6）提升+8.3点；0.6B和8B模型同样有增益。相比测试时计算的UQ-TTC基线，本方法在大多数设置下表现更好且仅需单次推理。

Conclusion: Self-Verified Distillation证明了大语言模型可以在无外部监督下通过自生成+自验证+自训练的方式持续自我改进，验证了多级级联验证策略在自训练数据筛选中的有效性。

Abstract: Can post-trained large language models (LLMs) further improve themselves using only unlabeled prompts, without external teachers or feedback from tools? We study this setting starting only from unlabeled seed questions with no ground-truth solutions, across three reasoning domains: math, science, and coding. We propose Self-Verified Distillation, a simple post-training refinement algorithm in which the model generates candidate solutions to these seed questions, filters them using prompt-based self-verification, and trains on the resulting self-curated dataset. Inspired by the UQ benchmark's use of multiple validators to screen candidate answers to hard unsolved questions, we adapt this validation-based filtering idea to self-training: the model filters its own generated solutions through a three-stage cascade of cycle-consistency, factuality, and correctness checks, accepting a solution only if it passes all stages with unanimous judge votes. We find that sampling more candidate generations and using a larger verification budget during training data construction produces higher-quality self-curated data and, in turn, better reasoning models. We then train Qwen3 models at multiple scales with Self-Verified Distillation and obtain gains across all three domains. For Qwen3-4B, our method improves aggregate held-out pass@1 by +16.7 points in math (AIME26 and HMMT), +11.1 points in science (GPQA Diamond and HLE), and +8.3 points in coding (LCBv5 and LCBv6), with gains also extending to 0.6B and 8B models. Compared to our test-time-only baseline (UQ-TTC), which improves performance by spending extra compute at inference time, Self-Verified Distillation achieves better performance in most settings while requiring only a single inference call at test time.

</details>


### [97] [Pretraining Data Exposure in Large Language Models: A Survey of Membership Inference, Data Contamination, and Security Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26133)
*Ziyi Tong,Feifei Sun,Le Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将数据污染和成员推断统一在预训练数据暴露（PDE）框架下进行综述，形式化定义了不同暴露层级，系统回顾了攻击与防御方法，并总结了经验发现与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模与预训练数据日益增长，训练数据集的规模和不可见性引发了对预训练数据暴露（PDE）的担忧。数据污染与成员推断这两个概念上相关但常被孤立研究的领域亟需统一框架，以保障评估完整性和保护隐私。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，正式将PDE定义并分层，系统梳理了数据污染和成员推断两个子领域的攻击与防御方法，并综合分析了现有实证研究结果。

Result: 提出了统一的PDE框架，涵盖不同暴露层级，汇总了现有攻击与防御方法，并识别了当前研究的不足与未来方向。

Conclusion: PDE是连接数据污染与成员推断的关键问题，现有研究仍存在开放挑战，未来需要更统一的评估标准和更有效的防御机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the predominant paradigm in NLP, advancing both research and industry. As model sizes and pretraining data grow, concerns about Pretraining Data Exposure (PDE) increase due to the scale and opacity of training datasets.
  PDE refers to determining whether specific data appeared in an LLM's pretraining corpus. It is critical for ensuring evaluation integrity and protecting privacy, intersecting two key areas: data contamination and membership inference. Though conceptually related, these areas have often been studied in isolation. This paper offers the first unified survey of both under the PDE framework. We formalize PDE across exposure levels, review attack and defense methods, synthesize empirical findings, and highlight open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [98] [CroCo: Cross-Lingual Contrastive Preference Tuning on Self-Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26293)
*Mike Zhang,Ali Basirat,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨语言对比偏好调优方法CroCo，利用英语偏好训练的多语言奖励模型对自生成响应进行排序，无需语言特定偏好标注即可提升多语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于奖励分数的对比性自生成响应偏好调优方法从英语扩展到多语言场景，探索无需语言特定偏好标注的跨语言偏好调优可能性。

Method: 使用基于英语偏好训练的多语言奖励模型，对自生成响应进行跨语言对比偏好调优（CroCo），涵盖单语言和多语言两种配对设置，并验证on-policy数据的重要性。

Result: 在EuroLLM-9B和Aya-3B两个模型、14种高/低资源语言上，结构化任务中6/7和4/7语言表现匹配或超越基线；开放生成任务中两种模型在11种语言上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 跨语言对比偏好调优无需语言特定偏好标注即可有效转移，但依赖on-policy数据，off-policy会降低收益，在线优化未优于离线变体。

Abstract: Prior work establishes that controlled contrastiveness between self-generated responses from large language models, set via reward scores, improves downstream preference tuning in English. We extend this method to multiple languages and evaluate two models across a total of 14 high and low-resource languages on a diverse set of tasks. Our central finding is that cross-lingual contrastive preference tuning on self-generations (CroCo) transfers without language-specific preference annotation. A reward model trained on English preferences (atop a multilingual base) produces useful within-language rankings across most languages, and pairing in either a monolingual or multilingual setting improves over each model on the majority of setups while preventing the catastrophic forgetting of supervised fine-tuning. We observe that the gains require on-policy data. Off-policy responses reduce the benefit and online preference optimization fails to improve over the offline variant. Specifically, on structured tasks, our method matches or exceeds the base in 6/7 languages for EuroLLM-9B and 4/7 settings for Aya-3B. On open-ended generation, both tuned models win against their respective base across 11 evaluated languages. Overall, we show promising directions for multilingual preference tuning.

</details>


### [99] [The Daily Dose: Workflow-Integrated Large Language Model Automation for Clinical Summarization and Trial Identification in Radiation Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26346)
*Jason Holmes,Federico Mastroleo,Mariana Borras-Osorio,Srinivas Seetamsetty,Satomi Shiraishi,Mirek Fatyga,Judy C. Boughey,Cornelius A. Thiels,William G. Breen,Daniel J. Ma,Daniel K. Ebner,David M. Routman,Brady S. Laughlin,Carlos E. Vargas,Samir H. Patel,Sujay A. Vora,Nadia N. Laack,Andrew Y. K. Foong,Wei Liu,Mark R. Waddle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于大语言模型的自动化临床摘要与临床试验识别系统（The Daily Dose），并在放射肿瘤科进行了为期1个月的混合方法评估。55名受访者中，83.6%每日或每周多次使用该系统，系统在可用性、满意度、感知有用性及未来使用意愿方面均获得积极评分，且整体满意度与感知时间节省显著正相关。问卷内部一致性极佳（Cronbach's α=0.97）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决放射肿瘤科日常工作中临床文档摘要和临床试验匹配耗时费力的问题，设计并评估一种LLM驱动的自动化系统，以提高临床工作效率。

Method: 采用混合方法评估，在系统部署1个月后开展横断面匿名临床医生调查。系统基于RadOnc-GPT每日自动生成医生专属邮件摘要，包含患者日程、EHR临床状态摘要及新患者/会诊的潜在相关临床试验推荐。主要结局指标为自报可用性、满意度、感知有用性、工作流影响、时间节省及持续使用意愿，使用Cronbach's α评估内部一致性信度。

Result: 55名受访者中，52人（94.5%）来自放射肿瘤科，38人（69.1%）为 attending physicians。83.6%每日或每周多次使用系统。可用性与满意度均分3.89（SD 1.04），感知有用性3.43（SD 1.24），影响力与未来使用3.80（SD 1.17）（5点量表）。整体满意度与感知时间节省正相关（p<.001），27%估计每日节省≥10分钟。问卷总Cronbach's α=0.97。

Conclusion: TDD系统在放射肿瘤科初步显示出良好的可用性、满意度和时间节省潜力，但感知有用性评分相对较低，提示后续需进一步优化系统功能以提升临床实用性。

Abstract: Objective: To describe the design and early clinical evaluation of The Daily Dose (TDD), an LLM-driven, automated clinical summarization and clinical-trial identification system integrated into routine radiation oncology practice. Design: Mixed-methods evaluation using a cross-sectional, anonymous clinician survey administered after 1 month of system deployment. Exposure: Daily automated delivery of physician-specific email summaries generated using RadOnc-GPT, including patient schedules, concise EHR-derived clinical-status summaries, and automated identification of potentially relevant clinical trials for new or consult visits. Main Outcomes and Measures: Primary outcomes included self-reported usability, satisfaction, perceived usefulness, perceived impact on workflow, time savings, and intention for continued use. Internal consistency reliability was assessed using Cronbach's $α$. Results: Among 55 respondents, 52 (94.5\%) worked in radiation oncology, and 38 (69.1\%) were attending physicians. Most participants (83.6\%) reported using TDD daily or several times per week. Mean (SD) scores were 3.89 (1.04) for usability and satisfaction, 3.43 (1.24) for perceived usefulness, and 3.80 (1.17) for impact and future use (5-point Likert scale). Overall satisfaction was positively associated with perceived time savings ($p < .001$). Participants reported variable time savings, with 27\% estimating $\geq 10$ minutes saved per day. The questionnaire demonstrated excellent internal consistency (overall Cronbach's $α$ = 0.97).

</details>


### [100] [RICE-PO: Turning Retrieval Interactions into Credit Signals for Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26352)
*Mingchen Li,Hansi Zeng,Zhuo Qian,Jiatan Huang,Hamed Zamani,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RICE-PO框架，通过将检索交互转化为局部学习信号，解决推理式检索Agent中潜在推理步骤的信用分配问题，在BRIGHT和BEIR上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索正从一次性匹配转向交互式推理，但训练此类Agent存在信用分配挑战：可执行动作（如查询）可直接评估，而潜在推理步骤不可直接观察且仅影响后续动作，导致基于最终奖励的分配不可靠。

Method: 提出RICE-PO框架：选择高不确定性可执行动作作为锚点，使用检索指标评估局部反事实分支，仅在推理-动作影响强且未来残差效应稳定时，将信用传播给潜在推理步骤。

Result: 在BRIGHT和BEIR数据集上，RICE-PO在相同检索器设置下一致优于基于提示的Agent和基于组的RL基线方法。

Conclusion: Agent-环境交互结构本身可以为训练基于推理的检索Agent提供有效的监督信号。

Abstract: Retrieval is increasingly moving from one-shot matching toward interactive reasoning, where language agents iteratively inspect evidence, reformulate queries, and search again. Training such agents raises a credit-assignment challenge: executable actions such as queries or summaries can be directly evaluated by the retriever, while latent reasoning steps are not directly observable and only affect future executable actions. This asymmetry makes outcome-level reward assignment unreliable, as the same final reward may credit reasoning steps that did not actually shape retrieval success. We propose RICE-PO, a critic-free policy optimization framework that converts retrieval interactions into localized learning signals. RICE-PO selects high-uncertainty executable actions as anchors, evaluates local counterfactual branches using retrieval metrics, and propagates credit to latent reasoning steps only when reasoning-to-action influence is strong and future residual effects are stable. On BRIGHT and BEIR, RICE-PO consistently outperforms prompt-based agents and group-based RL baselines under the same retriever setting. These results show that the structure of agent-environment interaction itself can provide useful supervision for training reasoning-based retrieval agents.

</details>


### [101] [In-Context Optimization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Gradient-Descent Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26356)
*Mingchen Li,Jiatan Huang,Chuxu Zhang,Liang Zhao,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明线性自注意力层可等效为对统一RAG目标的一步梯度下降，并基于此提出一种轻量级前向更新方法，在冻结RAG LLM上显著提升检索增强效果，接近测试时梯度适应但计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG将检索文档视为静态证据，忽略了其作为上下文信号对模型进行隐式适应的潜力。本文试图从上下文学习（ICL）与隐式梯度下降的理论联系出发，将RAG重新理解为上下文优化过程，并利用这一视角设计更高效的检索-推理交互机制。

Method: 1) 证明单层线性自注意力可等价于对统一线性化RAG目标的一步梯度下降，覆盖投影式和点积式检索接口；2) 在可控线性扩展下验证该对应的稳定性，并分析非线性架构下其特征分布依赖性；3) 提出轻量级前向更新方法，保持检索器和冻结LLM主干的参数不变，仅预测一个上下文条件化的更新量作用于生成端证据使用接口。

Result: 在7个QA基准、2种检索器和2种冻结LLM主干上，所提出的前向更新方法显著优于共享接口基线，可迁移到未见任务，并以极低的单查询成本逼近测试时梯度适应的性能。

Conclusion: RAG可被统一理解为上下文优化过程，线性自注意力与梯度下降的等价关系为设计高效检索增强机制提供了理论依据；所提出的轻量级前向更新方法在不微调模型的前提下有效提升了RAG性能，兼具实用性与泛化能力。

Abstract: In-context learning has recently been linked to implicit gradient descent in linear self-attention models, suggesting that context can induce a forward-pass update. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) also relies on context, but retrieved documents are usually treated as static evidence rather than signals for adaptation. We study RAG as an in-context optimization process. First, we show that one linear self-attention layer can implement one gradient-descent step on a unified linearized RAG objective covering both projection-based and dot-product retrieval interfaces. This gives an exact regime where retrieval-augmented prediction and in-context optimization coincide. We use this result not as a literal model of LLM computation, but as a guide for adapting the interaction between queries and retrieved evidence. We then test the boundary of this correspondence: it remains stable under controlled linear extensions, but becomes feature-distribution dependent under nonlinear architectures. Finally, we turn this view into a lightweight method for frozen RAG LLMs. The method keeps the retriever and backbone fixed, and predicts a context-conditioned update to a generator-side evidence-use interface. Across seven QA benchmarks, two retrievers, and two frozen LLM backbones, this forward-only update improves a shared-interface baseline, transfers to held-out tasks, and approaches test-time gradient adaptation at much lower per-query cost.

</details>


### [102] [Why LLMs Hallucinate on Structured Knowledge: A Mechanistic Analysis of Reasoning over Linearized Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26362)
*Shanghao Li,Jinda Han,Yibo Wang,Yuanjie Zhu,Zihe Song,Langzhou He,Kenan Kamel A Alghythee,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在推理任务中使用结构化外部知识时产生幻觉的机制，发现幻觉源于系统性的内部动态：注意力过度集中于捷径式结构线索，前馈表示无法有效锚定知识导致模型退回到参数记忆，且前馈层的语义锚定失败是幻觉的稳定特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在推理任务中可以利用结构化外部知识（如图、表），但在知识充足的情况下仍会产生幻觉输出，而背后的机制尚不明确。本文旨在探究这些机制，理解幻觉产生的根本原因。

Method: 通过分析大语言模型内部工作机制，重点关注注意力分布和前馈层表示两个维度，研究幻觉产生时的系统动力学特征。实验覆盖单跳图、多跳图以及表格等多种结构化知识格式。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 注意力过度集中于捷径式结构线索而非均匀分布在全上下文中；(2) 前馈表示未能锚定提供的知识，导致模型退回到参数记忆；(3) 幻觉与前馈层中的语义锚定失败稳定相关，而注意力分配则呈现更多的任务依赖性；(4) 这些机制模式在多种结构化知识格式中具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 幻觉并非随机噪声，而是由系统性的内部动力学导致。前馈层的语义锚定失败是幻觉的稳定标志，这一发现可用于跨结构化知识格式的幻觉检测。

Abstract: In many reasoning tasks, large language models (LLMs) rely on structured external knowledge, such as graphs and tables, which is typically linearized into sequential token representations. However, even when sufficient knowledge is available, LLMs can still produce hallucinated outputs, and the underlying mechanisms behind such failures remain poorly understood. We investigate these mechanisms and find that hallucinations arise from systematic internal dynamics rather than random noise. First, attention disproportionately concentrates toward shortcut-like structural cues rather than distributing across the full context. Second, feed-forward representations fail to ground the provided knowledge, causing the model to revert to parametric memory. Moreover, our results indicate that hallucination is consistently associated with failures in semantic grounding within feed-forward layers, while attention allocation exhibits greater task-dependent variability. Finally, we show that these mechanistic patterns generalize beyond single-hop graphs to multi-hop and tabular settings, enabling effective hallucination detection across structured knowledge formats.

</details>


### [103] [Cultural Value Alignment Via Latent Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26365)
*Trung Duc Anh Dang,Sarah Masud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，通过情景化行为探测（300个情境困境）和激活引导技术，从LLMs中提取潜在文化价值表征，并发现文化价值观在模型中呈现耦合编码结构，导致精确干预受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs常呈现同质化的文化视角，直接基于WVS的提示方法因安全对齐机制而无法触及模型的深层文化表征，需一种能绕过表层对齐的评估与干预框架。

Method: 1) 从抽象问答转向情景化行为探测，通过提取300个情境困境中隐式token概率来映射LLMs文化价值的潜在坐标；2) 采用激活引导（activation steering），在前向传播中调整内部对齐，无需重新训练。

Result: 不同LLMs的文化可适应性存在显著差异；发现一致的潜在纠缠现象，即对某一文化维度的干预会引发其他维度的偏移；文化价值以耦合结构编码，限制了精确对齐。

Conclusion: 建立了一个计算高效的文化引导框架，揭示了在全球价值观对齐中处理结构复杂性的挑战，表明LLMs中的文化价值呈现耦合编码特性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often exhibit homogenized cultural perspectives. While the World Values Survey (WVS) provides a gold standard for mapping human values, traditional direct prompting of LLMs on WVS often fails to access the model's latent cultural depth, leading to safety-aligned refusals or neutral responses. Here, we propose a generalizable framework for cultural evaluation and intervention that transitions from abstract queries to scenario-based behavioral probing. By extracting implicit token probabilities across 300 situational dilemmas, we bypass surface-level alignment to map the latent coordinates of LLMs cultural value. We further introduce activation steering to shift these internal alignments during the forward pass without retraining. Across multiple LLMs, we find substantial variation in adaptability and uncover a consistent phenomenon of latent entanglement, where interventions along one cultural dimension induce shifts along another. These results suggest that cultural values are encoded as coupled structures, limiting precise alignment. This work establishes a computationally efficient framework for cultural steering, highlighting the structural complexities when navigating global value with LLMs.

</details>


### [104] [Memory Architectures for Multi-Turn Text-to-SQL: A Benchmark and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26394)
*Ravi Kumar Tummalapenta,Suman Addanki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EnterpriseMem-Bench——一个包含300个会话、1400轮的多轮Text-to-SQL基准测试，覆盖三个企业领域。在五种前沿模型和五种记忆条件下评估发现：所有模型的零-shot多轮Text-to-SQL在第三轮时准确率归零；记忆架构复杂度不单调提升性能，工作记忆起主导作用；Claude Sonnet 4.6在SEC EDGAR上比4.5下降17-33pp（代际退化）；推理模式下Claude错误分布变为单模态（全为错误结果）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮Text-to-SQL对企业分析至关重要，但现有评估主要局限于单轮设定，缺乏专门的多轮基准测试来系统评估记忆机制对模型性能的影响。

Method: 程序化构建300个会话、1400轮的多轮基准，涵盖三个企业领域；设计五项记忆条件实现三路消融（工作记忆窗口大小、情景检索、语义增强）；提出每轮诊断指标MBS；在五种前沿模型上评估，所有Claude模型启用扩展思维以与GPT推理模型对等。

Result: (1) 无状态多轮Text-to-SQL在第三轮后所有模型执行准确率为零；(2) 记忆架构复杂度不单调提升性能，工作记忆主导，附加组件产生+14至-16百分点的模型/数据集依赖效应；(3) Claude Sonnet 4.6在SEC EDGAR上比4.5退化17-33pp；(4) 推理模式下Claude错误分布变为单模态（全为错误结果）。

Conclusion: 多轮Text-to-SQL远未解决，工作记忆是关键瓶颈，代际退化值得警惕，当前推理能力无法弥补记忆缺失——为后续研究提供了明确的方向（工作记忆优化、长上下文建模、鲁棒性评估）。

Abstract: Multi-turn Text-to-SQL is central to enterprise analytics yet remains predominantly evaluated in single-turn settings. We introduce EnterpriseMem-Bench, a multi-turn Text-to-SQL benchmark of 300 sessions and 1,400 turns built programmatically from three enterprise domains (BIRD financial, SEC EDGAR, Northwind), with deterministic ground truth and per-turn memory-critical annotation. We evaluate five frontier models -- GPT-5 mini, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, and Opus 4.6 -- across five memory conditions enabling a three-way ablation isolating working-memory window size, episodic retrieval, and semantic augmentation as independent effects. All Claude models are evaluated with extended thinking enabled to maintain parity with GPT reasoning models. We introduce the Memory Benefit Score (MBS) as a per-turn diagnostic metric. Four findings emerge: (1) stateless multi-turn Text-to-SQL collapses to zero execution accuracy by Turn 3 across all five models, even under reasoning; (2) memory-architecture complexity does not monotonically improve accuracy -- working memory dominates, and additional components produce model- and dataset-dependent effects from +14 to -16 percentage points; (3) Claude Sonnet 4.6 underperforms Sonnet 4.5 by 17-33pp on SEC EDGAR across conditions, a generational regression persisting under reasoning; (4) under reasoning, Claude error distributions become mono-modal -- every non-correct turn is a wrong-result error. We release the benchmark, agent, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [105] [Annotator Positionality as Signal: Psychometric Weighting for Anti-Autistic Ableism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26397)
*Naba Rizvi,Harper Strickland,Saleha Ahmedi,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种针对反自闭症歧视语言的偏差感知评估框架，发现LLMs在识别歧视语言时依赖表面关键词匹配而非上下文因素，且经常产生有害输出，对自闭症群体持有更消极的态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地用于决策任务，可能放大或压制某些观点，尤其在影响自闭症群体的高风险场景中引发担忧。先前研究已发现LLMs中存在与残疾相关的偏见，但尚不清楚它们如何概念化歧视（ableism）或在文本中检测歧视。

Method: 提出一个偏差感知评估框架，以心理测量加权、社群邻近的ground truth为基准，锚定标注者的立场特征。该框架比传统多数投票聚合更严格，后者显著且一致地低估了自闭症群体和自闭症接纳者的观点。

Result: LLMs经常产生有害输出，错误地将社群重新使用的语言标记为歧视性语言，且在评估工具被掩码时对自闭症人士表达更消极的态度。错误分析表明模型依赖表面关键词匹配而非上下文因素（如说话者身份、语言是促进群体团结还是造成外群体伤害）。

Conclusion: 传统多数投票聚合方法不足以评估LLMs中的歧视性偏差；需要更严格的、考虑社群立场和上下文因素的评估框架；LLMs在检测反自闭症歧视语言方面存在系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in decision-making tasks where they can amplify or suppress perspectives, raising concerns in high-stakes settings affecting autistic communities. While previous research has identified disability-related biases in LLMs, it remains unclear how they conceptualize ableism or detect it in text. We introduce a bias-aware evaluation framework targeting anti-autistic ableist language with a psychometrically-weighted, community-proximate ground truth anchored in annotator positionality. This framework constitutes a stricter standard than conventional majority-vote aggregation which significantly and consistently underweights autistic and autism-accepting perspectives. We find that LLMs frequently produce harmful outputs, mislabel community-reclaimed language as ableist, and express more negative attitudes toward autistic people when assessment instruments are masked. Our error analysis reveals that models rely on surface-level keyword matching rather than contextual factors such as speaker identity, and whether the language fosters in-group solidarity or inflicts out-group harm.

</details>


### [106] [Towards Just-in-Time Adaptive Feedback: Enhancing Student Learning via Knowledge-Grounded LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26405)
*Younghun Lee,Amir Bralin,Nobel Sanjay Rebello,Dan Goldwasser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，通过结合领域专家知识，在真实教学环境中为大学生提供自适应、即时（JiT）反馈。该框架收集学生的推理逻辑（策略论文），分析错误类型，并生成非侵入性反馈。在超1000名学生的大学课程中部署后，学生成绩相比以往学期提升超过80%。通过分析学习轨迹，验证了与LLM的迭代对话有助于将误解转变为正确理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究缺乏在真实教学场景中利用LLM提供即时（JiT）反馈的明确方法论，亟需一个可落地、可扩展的自适应反馈框架来提升学生学习效果。

Method: 1）收集学生书面推理逻辑（策略论文）；2）基于推理内容分析潜在错误类型；3）利用LLM结合领域专家知识，生成非侵入性反馈，澄清缺失或错误概念；4）在大型大学课程（N>1000）中部署并评估效果。

Result: 相比以往学期，学生成绩提升超过80%。学习轨迹分析表明，与LLM的迭代对话能有效帮助学生从误解转向正确理解。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效利用LLM和专家知识在真实教学场景中提供即时自适应反馈，显著提升学习效果，并具有可推广性。

Abstract: Educational interventions are effective tools for enhancing student learning. While Large Language Models (LLMs) allow for generating adaptive feedback at scale, current studies lack clear methodologies for providing Just-in-Time (JiT) feedback in authentic instructional settings. In this paper, we present a framework that provides adaptive feedback by grounding LLMs with domain-specific expert knowledge. Our approach collects written reasoning logic (strategy essays) from students, analyzes potential error types based on the content of that reasoning, and delivers non-intrusive feedback designed to clarify missing or incorrect concepts. We deploy this framework in a large-scale university course (N > 1000), where it improved student performance by over 80% compared to previous semesters. Lastly, we validate the framework's pedagogical utility by analyzing the learning trajectories; we demonstrate how iterative conversations with LLM facilitate shifting one's misconception to correct understanding.

</details>


### [107] [Probing Minimalist Phase Structure in LLMs: What Universal Dependencies Cannot Represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26431)
*Yuanhao Chen,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过设计基于最简方案(MP)相边界数量的wh-移位刺激，发现13个大型语言模型中的12-13个编码了UD标注无法捕捉的形式句法抽象（相边界梯度与相内部凝聚力），且这些表征在因果上活跃，表明分布预训练能诱导出超越基于标注探针的形式句法表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UD标注不编码形式句法抽象（如相边界或相内部凝聚力），基于UD的结构探针无法回答LLM是否编码这些抽象的问题，需要设计不受UD距离影响的新实验范式。

Method: 设计wh-移位刺激的三个条件（裸小句、不定式、限定句），按MP相边界数量排序，其中UD距离在各条件下保持不变；对13个来自4个家族的LLM进行结构探针评估，并辅以激活修补验证因果活性。

Result: 跨从句对上12/13模型呈现相计数梯度；从句内对上13/13模型呈现符号不对称性（由相内部凝聚力预测），且激活修补确认12/13模型的表征因果活跃。

Conclusion: 分布预训练能诱导出与形式句法抽象一致的表征，这些抽象超出了基于标注探针的检测范围；UD探针提供的是句法编码的下界而非上界。

Abstract: Structural probes train on Universal Dependencies (UD), which does not encode formal-syntactic abstractions such as phase boundaries or phase-internal cohesion. Whether large language models (LLMs) encode these remains an open question that UD-based probing cannot answer by construction. We evaluate structural probes on wh-movement stimuli where UD distances are invariant across conditions by design -- any non-zero effect therefore reflects structure beyond UD. The three conditions -- bare small clause, infinitival, and finite -- are ordered by the number of Minimalist Program (MP) phase boundaries the wh-element crosses.
  Across 13 LLMs from four families, we find a phase-count gradient on a cross-clause pair (12/13 models) and a 13/13 sign asymmetry on a within-clause pair whose UD distance is identical across conditions -- the latter specifically predicted by phase-internal cohesion, an MP abstraction invisible to UD by construction. Activation patching confirms the representations are causally active in 12/13 models. These findings suggest that distributional pretraining can induce representations aligned with formal-syntactic abstractions beyond the reach of annotation-based probing; UD-grounded probes provide a lower bound on syntactic encoding, not an upper bound.

</details>


### [108] [Vectors Are Not Neutral: Sensitive-Information Inference from Exported LLM Representations in Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26433)
*Weixin Liu,Bowen Qu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM摘要系统导出向量（如最终隐藏状态和均值池化表示）的隐私泄露风险。以电子健康记录中的种族信息为敏感标签，在临床出院摘要生成场景下进行审计，发现降低一个导出向量的可恢复性并不降低另一个。提出SurfaceLoRA方法，通过梯度反转判别器进行参数高效微调，可显著降低目标向量的敏感信息可恢复性，但不影响未定位的汇集向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM摘要系统生成的紧凑向量表示可能被下游组件用于检索、监控等任务，但这些向量可能在不同访问控制下处理，导致敏感信息推断的风险。现有研究缺乏对导出向量隐私风险的系统审计和针对性缓解方法。

Method: 以临床出院摘要生成为案例，使用EHR记录的种族作为敏感标签，审计最终提示令牌隐藏状态和均值池化表示两种导出向量。引入SurfaceLoRA方法，在指定导出向量上附加梯度反转判别器进行参数高效微调，在平衡五向探针协议下评估。

Result: 降低一个导出向量的敏感标签可恢复性不降低另一个。SurfaceLoRA将目标最终令牌向量的种族可恢复性降低至接近随机水平，同时保持摘要质量；但未定位的汇集向量可恢复性仍显著较高。

Conclusion: 隐私审计和缓解应针对实际保留或暴露给下游组件的具体向量工件进行，不同导出向量间的隐私风险不可互换。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) summarization systems may pass compact vector representations of private inputs to downstream retrieval, monitoring, audit, or analytic workflows. Even when source documents remain access-restricted, derived vectors may be handled under different access controls and still support sensitive-information inference, creating a residual information-disclosure risk. We study this issue in clinical discharge-summary generation as a high-stakes case study, using electronic health record (EHR)-recorded race as a controlled sensitive-label audit. We audit two artifacts that a system might retain or expose to downstream components: the final prompt-token hidden state and the mean-pooled prompt representation. Our results show that reducing recoverability of the case-study sensitive label from one exported artifact does not necessarily reduce recoverability from another. As a mitigation case study, we introduce SurfaceLoRA, an exported-vector-targeted parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that uses a gradient-reversal discriminator attached to a designated exported vector. Under a balanced five-way probing protocol, SurfaceLoRA reduces EHR-recorded race recoverability from the targeted final-token artifact toward chance while preserving summarization utility, yet recoverability remains substantially higher from untargeted pooled artifacts. These findings show that privacy auditing and mitigation should be performed on the exact vector artifact retained or exposed to downstream components.

</details>


### [109] [Targeted Remasking: Replacing Token Editing with Token-to-Mask Refinement in Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26436)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-to-Mask (T2M)重掩码机制，作为对LLaDA模型中Token-to-Token (T2T)编辑的零训练替代方案，通过将有嫌疑的错误token重置为掩码状态，让扩散模型在更干净的上下文中重新预测token，在12个基准测试中平均提升性能，数学任务上最高提升+5.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLaDA模型中的Token-to-Token (T2T)编辑机制存在三个根本性局限：它将错误检测与替换耦合在一起、可能用错误token污染生成上下文、以及训练-推理噪声不匹配（模型生成系统错误与训练时的随机扰动不一致）。

Method: 提出T2M重掩码机制，将疑似错误的token重置回掩码状态而非直接替换；设计了三种互补的错误检测策略：基于概率的、触发器镜像的和基于时序差分的；提供了统一的理论分析证明T2M能净化生成上下文、将系统推理错误转化回模型原生掩码噪声类型、实现延迟承诺以联合多位置优化。

Result: 在12个基准测试中全面评估，T2M在需要精确token级输出的任务上普遍提升性能，数学任务提升最大（CMATH +5.92%）。错误分析发现主要失效模式是"最后一英里token损坏"（正确推理但最终答案被损坏），T2M修复了59.4%的此类情况。

Conclusion: T2M作为T2T的零训练、即插即用替代方案，通过更干净的生成上下文和更合理的错误处理机制，在多项任务上取得显著提升，尤其适用于需要精确输出的场景。

Abstract: Discrete masked diffusion language models such as LLaDA generate text through iterative denoising, where mask tokens are progressively replaced with predicted tokens. LLaDA2.1 introduced a Token-to-Token (T2T) editing mechanism that accelerates generation by directly replacing committed tokens suspected of being incorrect. However, we identify fundamental limitations of T2T editing: it couples error detection with replacement, pollutes the generation context with potentially incorrect tokens, and introduces a train-inference noise mismatch where systematic model-generated errors differ from the random perturbations seen during training. We propose Token-to-Mask (T2M) remasking, a training-free, drop-in replacement for T2T editing that resets suspected erroneous tokens back to the mask state, allowing the diffusion process to re-predict them under cleaner context. We design and empirically validate three complementary error detection strategies -- probability-based, trigger-mirrored, and temporal-difference-based -- and provide a unified theoretical analysis showing that T2M remasking purifies the generation context, converts systematic inference errors back to the model's native mask noise type, and enables delayed commitment for joint multi-position optimization. Comprehensive experiments across 12 benchmarks spanning knowledge, reasoning, mathematics, coding, and instruction following show that T2M generally improves performance on tasks requiring precise token-level output, with the largest gain on mathematics (+5.92% on CMATH). Error analysis on CMATH reveals that the dominant failure mode is last-mile token corruption -- where correct reasoning produces a corrupted final answer -- and that T2M repairs 59.4% of such cases.

</details>


### [110] [Conv-to-Bench: Evaluating Language Models Via User-Assistant Dialogues In Code Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26440)
*Victor M. dos Santos,Andre C. Castro,Samuel L. de S. Toledo,Bruno M. L. Calura,Lisandra C. de M. Menezes,Raul C. R. Mata,Telma W. de L. Soares,Bryan L. M. de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Conv-to-Bench是一个多阶段框架，能够自动将真实的多轮用户-助手对话转化为结构化、可验证的需求检查清单，用于评估LLM性能。在编程领域，该框架生成的评估集与人工标准（如BigCodeBench）达到近乎完美的Spearman相关性（ρ=1.000），且计算开销显著更低。LLM-as-a-judge验证显示与人工标注高度一致（κ=0.705），消融实验表明指令中心提取比多轮交互更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估基准严重依赖劳动密集型专家策展，难以跟上LLM的快速扩展速度，存在可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出Conv-to-Bench多阶段框架，利用真实对话日志中的"指令演化"，将碎片化用户意图解构为整合后的指令和二值化评估标准，自动生成结构化需求检查清单。

Result: 在编程领域，评估集与BigCodeBench的Spearman相关性高达ρ=1.000，计算开销显著更低；LLM-as-a-judge主评估器与人工验证的Cohen's κ=0.705，达到较高一致性。

Conclusion: Conv-to-Bench提供了一种可扩展、低成本的新范式，能够在用户中心AI应用持续多样化的情况下维持高保真评估标准。消融实验还揭示了指令中心提取比多轮交互本身更稳健这一重要发现。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has outpaced the scalability of traditional evaluation benchmarks, which remain heavily dependent on labor-intensive expert curation. We address this bottleneck with Conv-to-Bench, a multi-stage framework that automatically transforms authentic multi-turn user-assistant dialogues into structured, verifiable requirement checklists. By leveraging the "instructional evolution" found in real-world conversational logs, our approach deconstructs fragmented user intent into consolidated instructions and binary evaluation criteria.
