Conclusion: 当前指令调优的视觉-语言模型需要更好地校准何时视觉上下文应参与词汇判断，而非默认将视觉输入视为有益信息。

Abstract: Visual inputs are often assumed to improve language understanding in multimodal models. We examine this assumption by asking whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish useful visual evidence from incidental image context in lexical judgments. We use human concreteness and imagery ratings because they span words with varying expected visual relevance, from abstract and low-imagery words to concrete and high-imagery words. We find that real-image contexts do not yield consistent gains and often hurt alignment with human ratings, most sharply when visual evidence is least relevant. Through probing and canonical correlation analysis, complemented by an attribution case study, we find that real-image contexts are associated with representational shifts and greater sensitivity to spurious visual cues, coinciding with weaker recoverability of the targeted lexical properties. We further show that instructing models to focus solely on textual content at inference time can reduce this degradation, with the clearest gains on these vulnerable subsets. Our findings suggest that current instruction-tuned VLMs need better calibration of when visual context should inform lexical judgments.

</details>


### [187] [Semantic Gradients Interactions in SSD: A Case Study in Racial Identity and Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27322)
*Felix Ostrowicki,Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出交互式语义差异（Interaction SSD）方法，扩展了监督语义差异模型，用于量化语义意义在不同调节变量（如群体、特质或条件）间的变化，并使这种变化可检验、可解释。通过在UC Berkeley仇恨言论语料库上的实验，验证了标注者种族身份对仇恨言论判断的调节效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督语义差异（SSD）方法能够估计语义梯度并解释词汇意义与结果变量之间的关系，但无法检验这种意义-结果关系是否在不同群体或条件下存在显著差异。作者希望开发一种能够建模并统计检验语义意义如何随调节变量变化的扩展方法。

Method: 提出Interaction SSD，在标准SSD基础上引入主语义梯度、交互梯度和条件梯度。主梯度捕获共享的语义变化模式，交互梯度捕获不同调节水平间的差异，条件梯度则给出各调节水平特有的语义映射。所有梯度均可通过标准SSD工具进行解释。

Result: 在UC Berkeley仇恨言论语料库上，模型检测到显著的调节效应：共享梯度区分了"去人性化敌意"与"反言论"；交互梯度揭示了不同种族身份标注者在哪些语义线索预示仇恨言论评分上存在较小但显著的群体差异。

Conclusion: Interaction SSD使调节化的意义-结果关系变得可统计检验和可解释，为计算语言学和社会科学中考察语义随群体或条件变化的研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We introduce interaction SSD, an extension of Supervised Semantic Differential that models how semantic meaning varies across moderators such as groups, traits, or conditions making this variation testable and interpretable. The method estimates a main semantic gradient, an interaction gradient, and conditional gradients, all interpretable through standard SSD tools. We illustrate it on the UC Berkeley Measuring Hate Speech corpus, testing whether annotator racial identity moderates hate-speech judgments of comments targeting people of color. The interaction model detects a significant moderation effect: the shared gradient contrasts dehumanizing hostility with counter-speech, while the interaction gradient reveals smaller group-linked differences in which semantic cues predict hate-speech ratings. Interaction SSD makes moderated meaning-outcome relationships statistically testable and interpretable.

</details>


### [188] [FinHarness: An Inline Lifecycle Safety Harness for Finance LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27333)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Yingguang Yang,Yancheng Chen,Jiayu Liang,Qian Li,Hanning Lu,Kefu Xu,Hao Zheng,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Peng,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinHarness 是一个内联安全防护框架，通过查询监控、工具监控和级联模块，在金融 LLM Agent 执行过程中实时拦截风险工具调用，将攻击成功率从 38.3% 降至 15.0%，同时以自适应路由方式节省 4.7 倍高级判读器调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边界过滤器容易遗漏中间轨迹中的不可逆工具调用，而事后 LLM 判读器只能在终止后审计，无法干预且成本随轨迹长度线性增长。

Method: 提出 FinHarness，包含三个组件：Query Monitor（融合单轮意图与跨轮漂移）、Tool Monitor（评估每次候选工具调用）、Cascade 模块（集成每步风险并自适应路由轻量/高级 LLM 判读器），将触发的风险因子作为事前证据重新注入 agent 输入，使其自主拒绝、重规划或批准。

Result: 在 FinVault 上，攻击成功率从 38.3% 降至 15.0%，良性批准率从 41.1% 降至 39.3%，高级判读器调用次数比始终使用高级判读器的消融实验减少 4.7 倍。

Conclusion: FinHarness 通过内联实时防护与自适应判读路由，有效兼顾金融 LLM Agent 的安全性（阻断攻击）与可用性（保留良性操作），且显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Finance LLM agents must simultaneously block prompt-induced unauthorized actions and approve legitimate multi-step business workflows. However, boundary filters often miss irreversible mid-trajectory tool calls, while post-hoc LLM judges perform auditing only after termination -- too late for intervention and at a computational cost that scales linearly with trace length. We present FinHarness, an inline safety harness that wraps a finance agent end-to-end with three components: a Query Monitor that fuses single-turn intent with cross-turn drift, a Tool Monitor that evaluates each prospective tool call, and a Cascade module that integrates per-step risk and adaptively routes verification between a lightweight and an advanced-tier LLM judge. Fired risk factors are re-injected into the agent input as ex-ante evidence, enabling the agent to refuse, re-plan, or approve on its own. On FinVault, routed FinHarness cuts ASR from 38.3% to 15.0% while largely preserving benign approval ($41.1\% \to 39.3\%$), and uses $4.7\times$ fewer advanced-judge calls than an always-advanced ablation.

</details>


### [189] [MATCHA: Matching Text via Contrastive Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27345)
*Siran Li,Ece Sena Etoglu,Carsten Eickhoff,Seyed Ali Bahrainian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有自动评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore）无法区分语义一致与矛盾文本，并提出MATCHA——一种通过双视角（接近黄金参考+远离对抗生成矛盾）来同时奖励语义一致性并惩罚矛盾的新型自动评估指标。在8个公开基准上，MATCHA优于流行指标，在TruthfulQA上相比ROUGE-L提升18.38%、相比BERTScore提升20.82%，且超越23个嵌入模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（基于Token重叠如ROUGE和基于嵌入如BERTScore）常将相互矛盾的文本分配几乎相同的分数，掩盖了底层错误，无法可靠评估LLM输出的语义一致性。

Method: 提出MATCHA，采用双视角方法：(i) 测量与黄金参考文本的语义接近度，(ii) 测量与对抗生成的矛盾反事实文本的语义距离，从而同时奖励语义一致并惩罚矛盾。

Result: 在8个公开基准（包括QA、图像描述生成、NLI、摘要、语义文本相似性）上优于流行指标；在TruthfulQA上相较ROUGE-L提升18.38%、相较BERTScore提升20.82%；在区分正确/错误陈述方面超越23个嵌入模型。

Conclusion: MATCHA在自动评估中有效识别并惩罚矛盾，揭示现有指标的固有缺陷，为LLM评估提供更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation is essential for understanding large language model (LLM) performance, yet today's go-to metrics, namely token-overlap scores (e.g., ROUGE) and embedding-based measures (e.g., BERTScore), often misjudge semantic similarity of documents. Our study shows that both token-overlap metrics and embedding-based metrics routinely assign nearly identical scores to texts that directly contradict each other, thereby potentially masking fundamental errors. We introduce MATCHA, an automatic metric that jointly rewards semantic agreement with a reference and penalizes contradictions. MATCHA employs a dual-view perspective that measures (i) proximity to the gold text and (ii) distance from an adversarially generated counterfactual contradiction. In eight public benchmarks, MATCHA outperforms popular metrics, compared with human annotations on question-answering, image caption generation, natural language inference, summarization, and semantic textual similarity tasks. On the TruthfulQA dataset (i.e., a dataset without a training set, where no embedding-based metrics could locally train on), this improvement in terms of matching texts with a reference reaches 18.38% over ROUGE-L and 20.82% over BERTScore. Both quantitative comparison and qualitative human assessments confirm the efficacy and validity of MATCHA and uncover fundamental weaknesses in pre-existing metrics. Compared with 23 embedding models, including top state-of-the-art ones, used as a metric similar to BERTScore, MATCHA remains the most accurate in distinguishing correct from incorrect statements solely based on a reference. Our code and metric are publicly available (https://github.com/Siran-Li/MATCHA).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [BrickAnything: Geometry-Conditioned Buildable Brick Generation with Structure-Aware Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26182)
*Zhengyang Ni,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrickAnything是一种几何条件自回归框架，能从多种3D表示生成可建造的砖块结构，通过点云作为统一接口、结构感知树状分词、偏好对齐后训练和自适应回滚等技术，实现高几何保真度和物理可建造性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有砖块生成方法要么依赖启发式优化（在目标3D形状不可行时失效），要么生成砖块序列时未显式建模底层3D几何和装配关系，缺乏既能保证几何重建又能满足离散部件约束和结构稳定性的统一方法。

Method: 提出BrickAnything框架：1）以点云作为统一几何接口；2）引入结构感知树状分词（Structure-aware Tree Tokenization），通过局部附着关系表示砖块结构，使序列生成更符合实际建造过程并减少无效中间状态；3）采用偏好对齐后训练（Preference-based Alignment Post-training）、有效性约束解码和自适应回滚（Adaptive Rollback）来提升可建造性目标（如稳定性和几何保真度）。

Result: 大量实验表明BrickAnything能生成几何忠实且物理可实现的砖块结构，所提出的树状分词相比传统排序策略有效减少了回滚和重新生成。

Conclusion: BrickAnything通过几何条件自回归建模和结构感知序列表示，成功解决了从3D形状生成可建造砖块结构的关键挑战，证明了基于序列生成的方法在离散部件约束和结构稳定性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Generating physically buildable brick structures from 3D shapes requires more than geometric reconstruction: the output must also satisfy discrete part constraints and structural stability. Existing brick generation methods either rely on heuristic optimization, which can break down when the target 3D shape does not admit a feasible structure under predefined constraints, or generate brick sequences without explicitly modeling the underlying 3D geometry and assembly relations. In this work, we present BrickAnything, a geometry-conditioned autoregressive framework for generating buildable brick structures from diverse 3D representations. BrickAnything uses point clouds as a unified geometric interface and predicts brick sequences that reconstruct the target shape under assembly constraints. To model structural dependencies among bricks, we introduce a structure-aware tree tokenization, which represents brick structures through local attachment relations. This formulation makes sequence generation more consistent with the physical construction process, and reduces invalid intermediate states. We further introduce preference-based alignment post-training, validity-constrained decoding and adaptive rollback to improve buildability objectives such as stability and geometric fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrickAnything produces geometrically faithful and physically realizable brick structures, and that the proposed tokenization effectively reduces rollback and regeneration compared with conventional ordering strategies.

</details>


### [191] [Can LLMs Introspect? A Reality Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26242)
*Shashwat Singh,Tal Linzen,Shauli Ravfogel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑大语言模型能检测并报告自身内部状态的观点，基于人类元认知研究的教训指出，当前行为证据无法区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配。通过重新审视两种评估范式，发现模型的成功实际上反映的是检测异常的一般能力或依赖输入特征，而非对内省状态的真正访问，因此现有证据不足以证明LLM具备元认知监控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑现有研究声称LLM能检测和报告自身内部状态（内省）的结论，指出需区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配，认为行为证据本身不足以建立强内省主张。

Method: 重新审视两种评估范式：（1）内部状态篡改检测任务——对比模型区分内部状态干预与输入操作的能力；（2）基于自身隐藏状态预测标签的任务——对比模型与仅访问输入的分类器的性能，并引入重新标注的控制设置以消除任务语义依赖。

Result: （1）模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入操控，其成功反映的是更通用的异常检测能力；（2）仅访问输入的分类器能达到与模型内省预测相当的性能；（3）在重新标注的控制设置中（模型必须依赖内部表征而非任务语义），模型表现接近随机。

Conclusion: 现有证据不足以证明LLM具备元认知监控（内省）能力，行为证据本身不足以支撑强内省主张。

Abstract: Can large language models detect and report their own internal states? A number of studies have argued that the answer to this question is yes. We argue, based on lessons from human metacognition research, that this conclusion may be premature: to be convinced of this conclusion we need to distinguish genuine introspection from pattern matching based on surface-level cues. Furthermore, we argue that behavioral evidence alone is inherently insufficient to establish strong introspective claims.
  We re-examine two recently introduced evaluation paradigms in light of this consideration. In the first paradigm, models are expected to detect whether their internal states have been tampered with. We find that models cannot reliably distinguish such interventions on their internal states from manipulations of the input, suggesting that their success in the original studies reflects their ability to detect anomalies more generally, as opposed to interventions on their internal states in particular. In the second paradigm we examine, models are tasked with predicting labels derived from their own hidden states. Here, we find that classifiers that only have access to the input achieve equivalent performance to the model's own in-context predictions, indicating that the original results do not conclusively demonstrate that the model has privileged access to its internal representations. We further introduce a relabeled control setting, where models cannot rely on the semantics of the task to solve it, and instead must rely on the internal representation; models perform closer to chance on this better-controlled version of the task. Taken together, these results indicate that current evidence is insufficient to establish that LLMs display metacognitive monitoring.

</details>


### [192] [Personalizing Embodied Multimodal Large Language Model Agents over Long-term User Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26256)
*Jeongeun Lee,Chanyoung Park,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出POLAR框架，通过多模态知识图谱记忆增强机制，使具身智能体能够利用长期用户交互中的个性化上下文信息，在跨多轮交互、多跳推理和用户上下文追踪等任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM具身智能体只能遵循通用指令或识别物体类别，无法利用长期交互中积累的个性化上下文信息，而真实场景中的目标往往通过隐含的先验交互来指定。

Method: POLAR将历史交互组织为多模态知识图谱，包含语义记忆（个性化上下文和视觉概念）和 episodic 记忆（具身体验如智能体轨迹），并通过检索相关记忆来指导当前任务执行。

Result: 在多种MLLM骨干和评估场景下，该记忆机制持续提升性能，尤其在跨多轮交互推理、多跳推理和用户上下文追踪任务上增益显著。

Conclusion: 多模态记忆增强是提升具身智能体长期个性化能力的关键，能够更有效地利用历史交互中积累的信息。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based embodied agents have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks in physical environments. However, personalized assistance requires more than following generic instruction or recognizing object categories. In real-world scenarios, the intended target is often specified only implicitly through prior interactions, requiring agents to leverage personalized context accumulated over time. In this work, we propose POLAR, a multiomodal memory-augmented framework for personalized embodied agents over long-term user interactions. POLAR organizes prior interactions into a multimodal knowledge graph that captures semantic memory for personalized context and visual concepts, and episodic memory for embodied experiences such as agent trajectories. To execute embodied tasks, POLAR retrieves relevant memories to interpret the current request and guide task execution. We evaluate POLAR across multiple MLLM backbones and diverse evaluation scenarios to study the role of memory in long-term personalization. Results show that the proposed memory mechanism consistently improves performance by enabling more effective use of information accumulated over prior interactions. The gains are especially pronounced when the agents are required to reason across multiple interactions, perform multi-hop inference, or tracking updates in user-specific context over time.

</details>


### [193] [Your Agents Are Aging Too: Agent Lifespan Engineering for Deployed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26302)
*Jianing Zhu,Yeonju Ro,John Robertson,Kevin Wang,Junbo Li,Haris Vikalo,Aditya Akella,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgingBench，一个用于评估和诊断AI智能体长期部署后性能衰退（即"智能体老化"）的纵向可靠性基准。通过对~400次运行、跨越8-200个会话的实验，发现智能体老化表现为压缩、干扰、修订和维护四种机制，且行为测试可能保持正常但事实精度已下降，指出需要生命周期评估和分阶段诊断修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅在部署首日（day-one）测试模型性能，忽略了AI智能体在长期运行中因交互历史压缩、记忆增长、事实修订和维护等操作导致的状态变化和可靠性衰退问题。

Method: 提出AgingBench基准，将智能体老化分为四种机制（压缩老化、干扰老化、修订老化、维护老化）；使用时序依赖图（temporal dependency graphs）和配对反事实探针（paired counterfactual probes）生成诊断画像，定位记忆流水线的写入、检索和利用三个阶段的问题。

Result: 在7个场景、14个模型、多种记忆策略、~400次运行（8-200会话）中，发现：行为测试保持正常但事实精度下降；派生状态跟踪可能在单一模型内急剧崩溃；同一错误答案因诊断画像不同需要不同的修复策略。

Conclusion: AI智能体的可靠部署不能仅依赖更强的初始模型，而是需要生命周期评估、机制级诊断和针对记忆流水线不同阶段的靶向修复。

Abstract: Long-lived AI agents are increasingly deployed as persistent operational systems, yet they are still evaluated like freshly initialized models. Day-one benchmarks miss a basic systems question: how long does an agent remain reliable after deployment? Even when model weights are frozen, an agent's effective state keeps changing as it compresses interaction history, retrieves from a growing memory store, revises facts after updates, and undergoes routine maintenance. Reliability therefore becomes a lifespan property of the full agent harness, not only a snapshot property of the base model. We introduce AgingBench, a longitudinal reliability benchmark for agent lifespan engineering: measuring not only whether deployed agents degrade, but what form the degradation takes and where repair should target. AgingBench organizes agent aging into four mechanisms: compression aging, interference aging, revision aging, and maintenance aging. To diagnose these failures, AgingBench uses temporal dependency graphs and paired counterfactual probes that produce diagnostic profiles for the write, retrieval, and utilization stages of the memory pipeline. Across 7 scenarios, 14 models, multiple memory policies, and both runner-controlled and autonomous agents, over ~400 runs spanning 8 - 200 sessions show that agent aging is not one-dimensional: behavioral tests can remain clean while factual precision decays; derived-state tracking can collapse sharply within a single model; and the same wrong answer can require different repairs depending on what the diagnostic profile points to. These results suggest that reliable agent deployment requires lifespan evaluation, mechanism-level diagnosis, and stage-targeted repair, not only stronger day-one models.

</details>


### [194] [Anchor: Mitigating Artifact Drift in Agent Benchmark Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26321)
*Maksim Ivanov,Abhijay Rana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anchor任务生成流水线，通过约束优化程序从领域专家规范中联合生成指令、环境、真值解和验证器，解决了企业级AI agent训练环境中指令与验证不一致的"artifact drift"问题。基于Anchor构建了ERP-Bench基准（300个长周期ERP任务），实验发现前沿模型在26.1%的试验中满足显式约束，仅17.4%达到最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业级AI agent训练与评估环境难以同时兼顾真实性、可验证性和规模；环境和任务创建过程中存在"artifact drift"问题（指令、环境、oracle和验证器由松散耦合的过程生成时，对任务要求产生分歧，导致环境不可解、可奖励破解或不一致）。

Method: 提出Anchor流水线：将领域专家对业务工作流的规范形式化为约束优化程序，从单一参数化规范联合生成自然语言指令、环境配置、求解器验证的ground-truth解和基于状态的验证器。改变参数可生成难度可控、有已知最优解的新任务，产生与环境无关的奖励（仅依赖于最终状态的业务正确性）。基于Anchor构建ERP-Bench基准（300个长周期采购与制造任务）。

Result: 生成参数可预测实际任务难度；前沿模型在26.1%的试验中满足显式任务约束，仅17.4%的试验达到完全最优解。

Conclusion: Anchor和ERP-Bench为构建可审计的经济价值导向的agent评估环境提供了具体方案。

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to complete valuable, long-horizon business operations tasks, but training and evaluation environments for enterprise work still struggle to balance realism, verifiability, and scale. Environment and task creation frequently suffers from a failure mode we call artifact drift: when instructions, environments, oracles, and verifiers are created by loosely coupled processes, they frequently disagree on what a task requires, producing environments that are unsolvable, reward-hackable, or inconsistent. We introduce Anchor, a task-generation pipeline that formalizes domain experts' specifications of business workflows into constraint optimization programs. From a single parametric specification, the pipeline jointly produces a natural-language instruction, environment configuration, solver-certified ground-truth solution, and state-based verifier. With Anchor, altering parameters yields new tasks with controlled difficulty and known optimal solutions, producing harness-agnostic environments whose rewards depend solely on end-state business correctness. We apply Anchor to produce ERP-Bench: a benchmark of 300 long-horizon tasks spanning procurement and manufacturing workflows in a production-grade ERP system. We find that generation parameters predict realized difficulty, and that frontier models satisfy explicit task constraints in 26.1% of trials but reach a fully optimal solution in only 17.4% of trials. Overall, we show that Anchor and ERP-Bench offer a concrete recipe for building auditable evaluation environments for economically valuable agent work. We release the task generator and ERP-Bench dataset at erpbench.ai

</details>


### [195] [OmniToM: Benchmarking Theory of Mind in LLMs via Explicit Belief Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26322)
*Adam Bawatneh,Sagar Sapkota,Amrit Singh Bedi,Santu Karmaker,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniToM是一个通过显式建模信念结构来评估大语言模型心理理论能力的基准。它从895个故事中提取22,343个标注的信念命题，通过信念提取和信念标注两阶段评估，揭示当前LLMs在将叙事事实转化为角色信念时存在知识访问和表征决策的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ToM评估仅关注最终问答结果，无法判断模型是否真正构建了底层的心智状态表征，尤其是在涉及分歧、演变或错误信念的场景中。

Method: 构建OmniToM基准：从ToMBench的895个故事中提取22,343个信念命题，通过人类校准的LLM辅助标注管道为每个信念标注七维模式标签（递归阶数、真值状态、知识访问、显式性、内容类型、心智来源、语境），采用两阶段评估（信念提取和信念标注）。

Result: 零样本评估揭示了一个特定于角色的信念追踪瓶颈：当前LLMs难以完成将叙事事实转化为角色信念和共享心智状态所需的知识访问和表征决策。

Conclusion: OmniToM揭示了现有LLMs在心理理论推理中的结构性缺陷，表明仅以最终答案评判会掩盖深层推理能力的不足，需要更细粒度的信念表征评估方法。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' knowledge, intentions, and emotions, is commonly evaluated in large language models (LLMs) using end-point question answering, where performance is judged solely by the final answer to a social reasoning query. This paradigm obscures whether the model actually constructs the underlying mental-state representations required for robust reasoning, particularly in scenarios involving divergent, evolving, or mistaken beliefs. In order to address this research gap, we introduce OmniToM, a benchmark that directly evaluates these representations by requiring explicit modeling of belief structures for all relevant actors within a narrative. These structures are composed of belief propositions: minimal statements of what an actor takes to be true about the world or another actor's mental state, allowing knowledge, intentions, emotions, and false beliefs to be analyzed in a common format. Models are evaluated in two stages: Stage 1: Belief Extraction, which extracts from the story the beliefs relevant to its social dynamics, and Stage 2: Belief Labeling, which assigns each belief a seven-dimensional schema label covering recursive order, truth status, knowledge access, explicitness, content type, mental source, and context. Built from 895 stories from the existing ToMBench story corpus and augmented with 22,343 labeled belief propositions, OmniToM uses a human-calibrated LLM-assisted annotation pipeline. Across diverse models in zero-shot evaluation, OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck: current LLMs struggle with the knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

</details>


### [196] [Managing Uncertainty in LLM-Generated Procedural Knowledge for Virtual Laboratory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26333)
*Polychronis Karpodinis,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种原型框架，通过结构化领域表示和LLM生成的状态转移样本来提取候选规程规则，将其转化为显式可检查的约束，用于修复不确定的规程步骤，以管理LLM生成的虚拟实验室规程知识中的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟实验室的规程编写成本高且费力，LLM可辅助生成实验规程，但其输出不可直接执行，存在步骤遗漏、顺序错误、逻辑不正确等问题，需要管理这些不确定性。

Method: 使用结构化领域表示和不确定的LLM生成状态转移样本，提取候选规程规则，将其转化为显式可检查的约束，并用这些约束修复不确定的规程步骤。

Result: 在涉及实验室仪器、容器、工具和物料转移操作的虚拟实验室领域展示了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于管理结构化交互环境中不确定的规程知识，不仅限于教育虚拟实验室，具有更一般的应用价值。

Abstract: Educational virtual laboratories can make experimental training more scala-ble, adaptive, and accessible, especially when students have limited access to physical laboratory facilities. However, authoring new simulated laboratory procedures remains costly: educators must describe new equipment, define how instruments and materials interact, and specify valid procedural flows that can be executed or assessed inside the virtual environment. Large lan-guage models can assist in this authoring process by generating detailed ex-perimental procedures, but their output should not be treated as directly exe-cutable plans. They may omit necessary actions, arrange steps in the wrong order, or produce instructions that are logically incorrect or incompatible with the laboratory equipment. This paper presents a prototype framework for managing uncertainty in LLM-generated procedural knowledge for virtu-al laboratory planning. The framework aims to reduce procedural uncertainty by using structured domain representations and uncertain LLM-generated state-transition samples to extract candidate procedural rules, transform them into explicit and inspectable constraints, and use them to repair uncertain procedural steps. Although the motivating domain refers to educational vir-tual laboratories, the underlying problem is more general: managing uncer-tain procedural knowledge for action planning in structured interactive envi-ronments. We illustrate the approach in a virtual laboratory domain involving laboratory instruments, containers, tools, and material-transfer actions.

</details>


### [197] [ScientistOne: Towards Human-Level Autonomous Research via Chain-of-Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26340)
*Rui Meng,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Chun-Liang Li,Palash Goyal,Mihir Parmar,Yiwen Song,Yale Song,Rajarishi Sinha,Parthasarathy Ranganathan,Burak Gokturk,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Evidence (CoE)可验证性框架及基于该框架构建的端到端自主研究系统ScientistOne，通过证据链追溯机制和四项完整性检查，在75篇论文的五个前沿研究任务上，相比现有基线系统实现零幻觉引用、完美分数验证和最高方法-代码对齐度，同时匹配或超越人类专家表现，并成功泛化到医学影像、3D感知等六个额外任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究智能体虽然能产出看似专业的解决方案和稿件，但存在表面评估无法检测的可验证性失败——包括捏造的引用、不可复现的分数以及方法描述与实现之间的不一致，亟需一个能够保证研究输出可追溯、可验证的框架。

Method: 提出三方面贡献：1) Chain-of-Evidence (CoE)框架，要求每个声明的证据来源可追溯；2) ScientistOne系统，在文献综述、方案发现和论文撰写全流程中内建证据链；3) CoE Audit事后审计，包含分数验证、规范违反检测、引用验证和方法-代码对齐四项完整性检查。

Result: 在涵盖五个系统和五个前沿任务的75篇论文中，所有基线系统均表现出至少一种系统性失败模式：幻觉引用率达21%、分数验证通过率低至42%、方法-代码对齐度在20%-80%之间。ScientistOne实现零幻觉引用(0/337)、完美分数验证(12/12)和最高方法-代码对齐度(14/15)，在五个任务上均匹配或超越人类专家。此外在医学影像、细粒度识别、3D感知和语言建模等六个额外任务上，ScientistOne在Parameter Golf上达到SOTA，在MLE-Bench任务上获得金牌而基线系统完全失败。

Conclusion: 通过证据链机制和系统化审计，可以显著提升自主研究智能体的输出可信度和可验证性，ScientistOne在保持高性能的同时彻底消除了幻觉引用等关键可靠性问题。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents produce competitive solutions and professional-looking manuscripts, yet their outputs contain verifiability failures undetectable by surface-level evaluation: fabricated citations, unreproducible scores, and method descriptions that diverge from the implementation. We address this through three contributions. First, Chain-of-Evidence (CoE), a verifiability framework requiring every claim to be traceable to its evidence source. Second, ScientistOne, an end-to-end autonomous research system that maintains evidence chains by construction throughout literature review, solution discovery, and paper writing. Third, CoE Audit, a post-hoc audit whose four integrity checks -- score verification, specification violation, reference verification, and method-code alignment -- apply uniformly to all systems. Across 75 papers spanning five systems and five frontier research tasks, every baseline exhibits at least one systematic failure mode: hallucinated reference rates reach 21%, score verification passes in as few as 42% of papers, and method-code alignment ranges from 20% to 80%. ScientistOne achieves zero hallucinated references (0/337), perfect score verification (12/12), and the highest method-code alignment (14/15), while matching or exceeding human expert performance on all five tasks. ScientistOne further generalizes to six additional tasks spanning medical imaging, fine-grained recognition, 3D perception, and language modeling, achieving state-of-the-art on Parameter Golf and gold medals on MLE-Bench tasks where baselines fail entirely.

</details>


### [198] [Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366)
*Xinpeng Wang,William Cao,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Zhe Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型幻觉检测中的中间层信号利用问题，提出了一种无需训练、计算开销极小的自动层选择方法FEPoID（第一有效本征维度峰值），能够一致性地选出最优或接近最优的层，并引入了一种简单的截断策略进一步增强幻觉相关信号，显著提升了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明幻觉相关信号在LLM的中间层比最终层更强，许多工作利用这一特性进行幻觉检测，但如何自动选择高性能层的问题尚未得到充分探索，缺乏原则性的自动化方法。

Method: 首先提出若干关于中间层为何出现幻觉信号的假设，并评估相应的自动层选择标准；发现这些标准均不能持续带来满意性能。随后提出新标准FEPoID（First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension），即本征维度的第一个有效峰值，用于自动识别最优层。此外，引入一种简单的截断策略来放大幻觉相关信号。

Result: FEPoID能够一致性地选出最优或接近最优的层，其表现优于此前提出的各种选择标准以及现有的幻觉检测基线方法；该方法无需训练，计算开销可忽略；引入的截断策略进一步显著提升了整体检测性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效的自动化中间层选择方法FEPoID，解决了幻觉检测中如何自动选择高性能层的问题，同时验证了截断策略对幻觉信号放大的有效性。

Abstract: Recent studies on hallucination detection have shown that hallucination-related signals are more strongly encoded in intermediate layers than in the final layer of large language models (LLMs). Although a growing body of work has sought to exploit this property for hallucination detection, how to automate the selection of high-performing layers remains underexplored, and principled methods for this purpose are still lacking. To address this gap, we first propose several hypotheses for why such signals emerge in intermediate layers and evaluate corresponding criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse LLM architectures, scales, and tasks, covering both question answering and summarization hallucination detection benchmarks. However, we find that none of these criteria consistently delivers satisfactory performance. We therefore propose a new selection criterion, First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension (FEPoID), which consistently identify optimal or near-optimal layers and outperforms both the aforementioned criteria and existing hallucination detection baselines. FEPoID is training-free and incurs negligible computational overhead. In addition, we study the generation behaviors of LLMs and introduce a simple yet effective truncation strategy, which further amplifies hallucination-related signals and substantially improves overall detection performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git

</details>


### [199] [Exploiting Local Dynamics Regularity for Reusable Skills in Offline Hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26371)
*Sarthak Dayal,Abhinav Peri,Carl Qi,Claas Voelcker,Alexander Levine,Caleb Chuck,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CARL算法，通过对比学习对齐全局上下文与局部动作序列，实现可复用技能发现，在OGBench基准上提升HIQL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次强化学习（HRL）中获取真正可复用的技能仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 利用局部动力学直觉，通过对比学习对齐全局上下文与其所需的动作序列，学习哪些技能可复用及在何处复用。

Result: 在复杂类人环境中实现了有意义的技能聚类，集成到HIQL后在OGBench基准上取得性能提升。

Conclusion: CARL能够有效发现可复用技能，并提升下游HRL算法的性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) promises to solve long-horizon Reinforcement Learning (RL) tasks more efficiently than non-hierarchical counterparts by discovering and reusing temporally-extended skills. However, obtaining skills that are actually reusable remains an open challenge. Towards this end, we focus on abstractions that exploit the intuition of local dynamics: local transitions in different global contexts require similar kinds of action sequences. By aligning these contexts with the action sequences they require, we are able to learn which skills to reuse and where to reuse them. In principle, this information should benefit many HRL algorithms, where high-level policies have to reason about the low-level skills they use. The resulting algorithm CARL (Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control) shows both qualitative clustering of meaningful skills in complex humanoid environments and improved downstream performance on the OGBench benchmark when integrated with HIQL.

</details>


### [200] [Advancing Creative Physical Intelligence in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26396)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Jeonghwan Kim,Emre Can Acikgoz,Bingxuan Li,Kunlun Zhu,Jiateng Liu,Aditi Tiwari,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-CreativityBench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在开放式环境中基于视觉线索的创造性工具使用能力。研究发现当前LMMs主要因缺乏持续的基础探索而表现不佳，为此提出基于功能可供性偏好的对齐方法（DPO），结合知识库监督，显著减少了幻觉和接地错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要测试模式识别和问答能力，但未能评估模型在开放式环境中发现非显而易见的、物理可行的视觉解决方案的创造力，而这是人类智能的核心特征。

Method: 1）构建MM-CreativityBench基准，包含场景图像、候选实体及其部件结构化视图，支持细粒度交互评估；2）提出基于功能可供性偏好的对齐方法，将创造性工具使用建模为偏好学习问题，使用DPO鼓励模型偏好基于视觉证据的属性-可供性推理；3）引入基于可供性知识库的监督，引导实体探索和多轮规划。

Result: 当前LMMs在基准上表现不佳，主要失败模式包括忽略相关实体、未充分检查关键部件、产生非图像基础的幻觉。所提方法在正确选择实体和部件上取得一致提升，并大幅减少幻觉和接地相关错误。

Conclusion: 模型缺乏的是持续的接地探索能力而非生成能力，通过偏好学习和知识库监督可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have rapidly advanced in perception and reasoning; however, it remains unclear whether these capabilities generalize to discovering visually grounded solutions in open-ended environments, beyond pattern recognition. In such settings, intelligence requires more than answering well-posed questions: it involves identifying how elements in a scene can be repurposed in non-obvious yet physically feasible ways. This form of creative problem-solving is central to human intelligence, but remains largely untested in current benchmarks. To evaluate this ability, we introduce MM-CreativityBench, a benchmark for affordance-grounded creative tool use in visually rich, physically constrained environments. Each instance presents a scenario image with structured views of candidate entities and their parts, enabling fine-grained, interactive evaluation of how models iteratively inspect the scene, identify relevant affordances, and compose visually and physically grounded solutions. Our experiments show that current LMMs often fall short, not due to lack of generative capability, but because they do not sustain grounded exploration. Models often overlook relevant entities, under-examine critical parts, or hallucinate attributes not grounded in the image. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose affordance-grounded alignment, which casts creative tool use as a preference learning problem. Using Direct Preference Optimization, we encourage models to prefer attribute-affordance reasoning grounded in visual evidence over hallucinated alternatives. In addition, we incorporate supervision derived from an affordance knowledge base to guide broader entity exploration and multi-turn planning. Our results show consistent gains in selecting the correct entities and parts, while substantially reducing hallucination and grounding-related errors.

</details>


### [201] [From Static Context to Calibrated Interactive RL: Mitigating Distribution Shift in Multi-turn Dialogue with Aligned Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26403)
*Xiaohua Wang,Jiakang Yuan,Zisu Huang,Muzhao Tian,Changze Lv,Kaitao Song,Tao Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文理论证明了静态上下文强化学习和基于提示模拟器的交互式强化学习在对话系统中均受限于上下文分布偏移问题（该问题随轮次呈二次方累积），并提出校准交互式强化学习框架，通过联合交互式学习与模拟器对齐来减小模拟-真实差距，实验表明该方法能有效缓解两种分布偏移并达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究社区长期目标是开发高度交互的基于LLM的对话智能体，但现有方法（静态上下文RL和基于提示模拟器的交互式RL）存在根本性局限，即上下文分布偏移问题，这严重降低了对话质量。

Method: 提出校准交互式强化学习（Calibrated Interactive RL）统一框架，将交互式RL与模拟器对齐相结合，通过对齐模拟器与人类交互模式来减小模拟-真实差距，缓解随轮次二次方累积的分布偏移。

Result: 在多个对话任务上验证：1）交互式RL通过缓解策略分布偏移显著优于静态上下文基线；2）使用所提对齐方法校准模拟器能进一步弥合模拟-真实差距，实现最先进的下游性能。

Conclusion: 理论分析与实验均证明了上下文分布偏移对对话系统的危害，所提出的校准交互式RL框架能有效应对策略诱导偏移和模拟器诱导偏移两种来源的分布偏移问题。

Abstract: A long-standing goal of the research community is to develop highly interactive LLM-based dialogue agents. Recent research focuses on optimizing policies based on fixed offline logs (Static Context RL) or using a prompt-based simulator (Interactive RL). In this work, we theoretically show that both paradigms are fundamentally limited by context distribution shift--a mismatch between dialogue histories observed during training and those encountered in real conversations. This shift compounds quadratically over turns and severely degrades dialogue quality. Specifically, we attribute this shift to two distinct sources: (i) policy-induced shift, arising from training on static histories rather than self-generated trajectories; and (ii) simulator-induced shift, stemming from discrepancies between simulated and real human behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose Calibrated Interactive RL, a unified framework that couples interactive RL with simulator alignment. By aligning the simulator with human interaction patterns, our approach reduces the sim-to-real gap and mitigates compounding distribution shifts. Experiments across multiple dialogue tasks confirm our theoretical analysis: (i) Interactive RL significantly outperforms the Static Context baseline by mitigating policy distribution shift; and (ii) calibrating simulators with our alignment method further bridges the sim-to-real gap, yielding state-of-the-art downstream performance.

</details>


### [202] [The MiniMax-M2 Series: Mini Activations Unleashing Max Real-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26494)
*MiniMax,:,Aili Chen,Aonian Li,Baichuan Zhou,Bangwei Gong,Binyang Jiang,Boji Dan,Changqing Yu,Chao Wang,Cheng Ma,Cheng Zhong,Cheng Zhu,Chengjun Xiao,Chengyi Yang,Chengyu Du,Chenyang Zhang,Chi Zhang,Chuangyi Huang,Chunhao Zhang,Chunhui Du,Chunyu Zhao,Congchao Guo,Da Chen,Deming Ding,Dianjun Sun,Dongyu Zhang,Enhui Yang,Fei Yu,Guang Zheng,Guodong Zheng,Guohong Li,Haichao Zhu,Haigang Zhou,Haimo Zhang,Han Ding,Hao Zhang,Haohai Sun,Haolin Lyu,Haonan Lu,Haoyu Wang,Huajie Shi,Huiyang Li,Jiacheng Chen,Jian Zhang,Jiaqi Zhuang,Jiaren Cai,Jiaxin Pan,Jiayao Li,Jiayuan Song,Jichuan Zhang,Jie Wang,Jihao Gu,Jin Zhu,Jingwei Dong,Jingyang Li,Jingyu Zhang,Jingze Zhuang,Jinhao Tian,Jinli Liu,Jinyi Hu,Jun Tao,Jun Zhang,Junbin Ruan,Junhao Xu,Junjie Yan,Junteng Liu,Junxian He,Kang Xu,Ke Ji,Ke Yang,Kecheng Xiao,Keyu Duan,Keyu Li,Le Han,Letian Ruan,Li Yuan,Lianfei Yu,Liheng Feng,Lijie Mo,Lin Li,Lingye Bao,Lingyu Yang,Lingyuan Zhou,Loki,Lu Chen,Lunbin Ceng,Ming Li,Ming Zhong,Mingliang Tao,Mingyuan Chi,Mujie Lin,Nan Hu,Ningxin Chen,Peiyin Zhu,Peng Gao,Pengcheng Gao,Pengfei Li,Penglin Li,Pengyu Zhao,Qibin Ren,Qidi Xu,Qihan Ren,Qile Li,Qin Wang,Quanliang Chen,Qunhong Ceng,Rong Tian,Rui Dong,Ruitao Leng,Ruize Zhang,Shanqi Liu,Shaoyu Chen,Sheng Jia,Shun Yao,Shuoran Zhao,Shuqi Yu,Sichen Li,Sicheng Pan,Songquan Zhu,Tengfei Li,Tian Xie,Tiancheng Qin,Tianrun Liang,Wei Liu,Weiqi Xu,Weitao Li,Weixiang Chen,Weiyu Cheng,Weiyu Zhang,Wenhu Chen,Wenqian Zhao,Xiancai Chen,Xiangjun Song,Xiangyuan Wang,Xiao Luo,Xiao Su,Xiaobo Li,Xiaodong Han,Xiaojie Wu,Xihao Song,Xingyi Han,Xinyu Guan,Xuan Lu,Xun Zou,Xunhao Lai,Xutong Li,Yan Gong,Yang Wang,Yang Xu,Yangsen Wang,Ye Tang,Yicheng Chen,Yinran Qiu,Yiqi Shi,Yiting Guo,Yiwen Huang,Yixuan Wang,Yongyi Hu,Yu Gao,Yu Zhang,Yuanxiang Ying,Yuanzhen Zhang,Yubo Wang,Yuchen Song,Yufeng Yang,Yuhang Meng,Yuhang Miao,Yuhao Li,Yujie Liu,Yulin Hu,Yunan Huang,Yunji Li,Yunyi Huang,Yusen Zhang,Yusu Hong,Yutao Xie,Yutong Zhang,Yuwen Liao,Yuxuan Shi,Yuze Wenren,Zebin Li,Zehan Li,Zejian Luo,Zeyu Jin,Zeyuan Sun,Zhanpeng Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhendong Li,Zhengmao Zhu,Zhengyuan Peng,Zhenhua Fan,Zhi Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Zhiheng Lv,Zhikang Xu,Zhitao He,Zhiwei He,Zhongyuan Li,Zibo Gao,Zijia Wu,Zijian Song,Zijian Zhou,Zijun Sun,Zishan Huang,Ziying Chen,Ziyue Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniMax-M2系列是基于MoE架构的大语言模型，总参数229.9B但每token仅激活9.8B参数。该系列专为智能体部署设计，核心包括三项技术：(i) 基于智能体驱动的数据管道生成大规模可验证轨迹；(ii) Forge——一个可扩展的智能体原生RL系统；(iii) M2.7检查点首次实现自我进化——自主调试训练并修改自身框架。在智能体编程、深度搜索、办公任务和推理基准上达到了前沿水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索"小型激活参数能否释放最大现实智能"这一核心原则，并解决现有大语言模型在智能体部署场景中的效率与性能瓶颈。

Method: 采用MoE架构（总参229.9B/激活9.8B），构建三项核心组件：(i) 智能体驱动的数据管道，在可执行工作空间和奖励机制下生成大规模编码和协作轨迹；(ii) Forge系统，支持长序列智能体轨迹的RL训练，包含窗口FIFO调度、前缀树合并、推理优化及训练-推理-智能体三阶段解耦；(iii) M2.7实现自我进化，自主调试训练并修改scaffold。

Result: 在智能体编码、深度搜索、办公任务和推理基准上，仅激活9.8B参数即达到与更大模型竞争的前沿性能水平。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的MoE架构、智能体数据管道和原生RL系统，MiniMax-M2系列证明了小激活参数同样可以释放强大的现实智能，为高效智能体部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce the MiniMax-M2 series, a family of Mixture-of-Experts language models built around the principle that mini activations can unleash maximum real-world intelligence. The flagship M2 contains 229.9B total parameters with only 9.8B activated per token. Designed end-to-end for agentic deployment, the M2 series rests on three components: (i) agent-driven data pipelines producing large-scale, verifiable trajectories across agentic coding and agentic cowork, each grounded in an executable workspace and an artifact-aligned reward; (ii) Forge, a scalable agent-native RL system that adapts to long-horizon agent trajectories, paired with windowed-FIFO scheduling, prefix-tree merging, inference optimization, and a clean training-inference-agent decoupling that supports both white-box and black-box agents; (iii) the latest M2.7 checkpoint takes an early step toward self-evolution -- autonomously debugging training runs and modifying its own scaffold. Across M2 through M2.7, this combination translates a mini-activation footprint into frontier-tier performance on agentic coding, deep search, office-task, and reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [203] [PolyFusionAgent: A Multimodal Foundation Model and Autonomous AI Assistant for Polymer Property Prediction and Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26543)
*Manpreet Kaur,Xingying Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolyFusionAgent是一个结合多模态聚合物基础模型（PolyFusion）和文献驱动设计智能体（PolyAgent）的交互式框架，通过对齐聚合物的序列、拓扑、3D几何和指纹等多种视图，实现跨化学空间的属性预测、逆设计生成和基于文献证据的科学推理，形成闭环聚合物发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物发现受限于巨大的化学设计空间和结构、属性、先验知识的碎片化表示，导致许多AI模型与物理和实验现实脱节，无法支持直接可行的设计决策。

Method: 提出PolyFusionAgent框架：1) PolyFusion多模态基础模型，对齐聚合物的序列、拓扑、3D几何和指纹等多视图信息，学习跨化学空间的共享潜在表征；2) PolyAgent工具增强型设计智能体，通过检索聚合物文献证据，将预测和逆设计与可验证的科学推理联系起来，形成闭环设计工作流。

Result: 改善了热物理性质预测性能，实现了超越参考设计空间的化学有效且结构新颖的聚合物的性质条件生成，并能基于文献证据提出、评估和上下文化设计假设。

Conclusion: PolyFusionAgent实现了结合大规模表征学习、多模态化学知识和可验证科学推理的交互式、基于证据的聚合物发现。

Abstract: Polymer discovery is central to fields ranging from energy storage to biomedicine, but it is hindered by an astronomically large chemical design space and fragmented representations of structure, properties, and prior knowledge. This fragmentation leaves many AI models disconnected from physical and experimental reality, restricting their ability to support directly actionable design decisions. Here we introduce PolyFusionAgent, an interactive framework coupling a multimodal polymer foundation model (PolyFusion) with a tool-augmented, literature-grounded design agent (PolyAgent). PolyFusion aligns complementary polymer views including sequence, topology, 3D geometry, and fingerprints across millions of polymers to learn a shared latent space transferable across chemistries and data regimes, improving thermophysical property prediction and enabling property-conditioned generation of chemically valid, structurally novel polymers beyond the reference design space. PolyAgent closes the design loop by linking prediction and inverse design with evidence retrieval from the polymer literature, proposing, evaluating, and contextualizing hypotheses with explicit precedent in one workflow. Together, PolyFusionAgent enables interactive, evidence-linked polymer discovery combining large-scale representation learning, multimodal chemical knowledge, and verifiable scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [204] [MedGuideX: Internalizing Decision Logic from Executable Guidelines into Large Language Models for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26567)
*Yuhao Shen,Lang Cao,Simo Du,Yuqing Wang,Juexiao Zhou,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedGuideX方法，通过将临床实践指南转化为可执行决策逻辑并生成事实与反事实问答数据来微调医疗大语言模型，在四个临床推理基准上平均准确率提升10.28%，并生成医生偏好的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将临床实践指南作为自由文本训练数据或检索来源，未能充分利用其程序化决策结构，导致模型对指南结构化决策逻辑的利用不足。

Method: 提出指南派生训练流程：将CPG推荐转化为可执行临床决策逻辑，据此生成事实性和反事实性问答数据，教导模型掌握指南支持的决策以及不同患者条件下决策如何变化，随后在生成数据上后训练医学LLM得到MedGuideX。

Result: 在四个临床推理基准上，MedGuideX平均准确率相对提升10.28%；医生评估表明其在忠实性、有效性、完整性和清晰度方面更能恢复临床推理步骤并生成医生偏好的理由。

Conclusion: 来自CPG的可执行决策逻辑可以转化为可扩展的监督信号，用于构建可靠的医学大语言模型。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode evidence-based decision logic that clinicians apply by evaluating patient variables, conditional criteria, and recommendation rules. However, existing methods often use CPGs as free-text training data or retrieval sources, underutilizing their procedural decision structure. To better exploit this structure, we introduce a guideline-derived training pipeline that transforms CPG recommendations into executable clinical decision logic and uses it to generate factual and counterfactual question-answering data. Theses data teach models both guideline-supported decisions and how decisions change under different patient conditions. Post-training a medical LLM on the generated data yields MedGuideX. Across four clinical reasoning benchmarks, MedGuideX achieves a 10.28% relative improvement in average accuracy. Physician evaluation further shows that MedGuideX better recovers clinician authored reasoning steps and produces physician-preferred rationales in faithfulness, validity, completeness, and clarity. Overall, our results show that executable decision logic from CPGs can be transformed into scalable supervision for building reliable medical LLMs.

</details>


### [205] [AGORA: Adapter-Grounded Observation-Action Retention for Inference-Free Prompt Compression in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26596)
*Haoran Zhang,Zhaohua Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体的token级抽取式压缩存在"动作语法破坏"失效模式（动作语义token因自信息量低被通用压缩器删除），本文提出AGORA——一种推理无关的步骤级压缩器，结合结构提示解析器、格式/近因关键内容保留层和基于反事实下一动作变化标签训练的125M参数相关性评分器，在9个细胞中8个保留≥75%的非压缩性能，实现1.0-11.5倍自适应端到端压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token级抽取式压缩器在LLM智能体场景中完全失效（17个实验单元平均奖励≤0.05），原因是它们会删除携带动作语义的token（如标识符、括号、动作动词），导致环境拒绝执行残存动作，这种失效模式被称为"动作语法破坏"。

Method: 提出AGORA：(1) 结构提示解析器，用于步骤级压缩；(2) 始终保留层，保护格式和近因关键内容；(3) 125M参数相关性评分器，基于反事实下一动作变化标签训练，约2ms/步，无需每步调用LLM。

Result: AGORA是9个实验单元中唯一在8个单元上保留≥75%非压缩性能的方法（例外的一个为73%）；消融实验显示结构保留层是质量主导因素，学习型评分器在固定保留比率下实现1.0-11.5倍自适应端到端压缩。

Conclusion: 步骤级压缩优于token级压缩是解决LLM智能体上下文压缩问题的正确方向；结构保留层和基于反事实学习的重要性评分器是实现高质量压缩的关键组件。

Abstract: The token-level extractive compressors widely used for general LM context are structurally inappropriate for LLM agents: across 17 (env, backbone, method) cells spanning two independent token-level method families, every cell collapses to mean reward <= 0.05 despite 1.3-13.3x realized compression. We name and characterize this failure mode as action-grammar destruction -- the tokens carrying action semantics (identifiers, brackets, action verbs) are exactly those self-information ranks lowest, so a general-purpose compressor reliably removes them and the environment rejects the residual. The diagnosis points to step-granularity compression. We introduce AGORA, an inference-free step-level compressor combining a structural prompt parser, an always-keep floor for format- and recency-critical content, and a 125M-parameter relevance scorer trained on counterfactual next-action-change labels (~2ms/step, zero per-step LLM toll). Across the compared inference-free and LLM-based methods, AGORA is the only one retaining >= 75% uncompressed performance in 8 of 9 cells (with the lone exception at 73%); a four-way component ablation isolates the structural floor as the dominant quality lever and the learned scorer as the source of 1.0-11.5x adaptive end-to-end compression from a single fixed keep ratio.

</details>


### [206] [Tail-Aware HiFloat4: W4A4 Post-Training Quantization for Wan2.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26628)
*Zhanfeng Feng,Shuai Guo,Xin Di,Long Peng,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Aware HiFloat4方法，通过适配ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程至Wan2.2，采用W4A4 HiFloat4伪量化主线性层、保留高精度敏感边界模块、引入激活尾部感知的百分位校准模块构建通道掩码，并结合紧凑的PTQ状态恢复，在保持HiFloat4运行算术和采样流程不变的前提下减少校准离群值影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低比特文本到视频生成量化挑战中，需要在不改变运行时HiFloat4算术和采样流程的前提下，降低校准离群值对量化效果的影响。

Method: 将ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程适配至Wan2.2，对Wan2.2变换器模块中的主线性层采用W4A4 HiFloat4伪量化，保留高精度敏感边界模块，并引入激活尾部感知的百分位校准模块用于通道掩码构建，同时结合紧凑的PTQ状态恢复。

Result: 该方法在保持HiFloat4运行时算术和采样流程不变的前提下，有效减少了校准离群值的影响，实现了低比特文本到视频生成的高效量化。

Conclusion: Tail-Aware HiFloat4通过激活尾部感知的百分位校准和紧凑的PTQ状态恢复，成功适配Wan2.2模型，为低比特文本到视频生成量化提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: This report describes Tail-Aware HiFloat4, our submission to the low-bit text-to-video generation quantization challenge. Our method adapts the public ViDiT-Q post-training quantization pipeline to Wan2.2 under the HiFloat4 numerical format. We quantize the main linear layers in both Wan2.2 transformer modules with W4A4 HiFloat4 fake quantization, keep numerically sensitive boundary modules in high precision, and introduce an activation-tail-aware percentile calibration module for channel-mask construction. Together with compact PTQ-state restoration, this design reduces the influence of rare calibration outliers while keeping the runtime HiFloat4 arithmetic and sampling pipeline unchanged.

</details>


### [207] [UnityMAS-O: A General RL Optimization Framework for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26646)
*Yiqun Chen,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Qi Liu,Zechun Niu,Bin Zhang,Haitao Li,Rui Li,Lingyong Yan,Jinyuan Feng,Biqing Qi,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UnityMAS-O是一个面向LLM多智能体系统的通用强化学习优化框架，将完整工作流作为优化单元，支持逻辑角色与物理模型解耦、多层级奖励分配，并在检索QA、迭代搜索和代码生成任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统依赖手动编排（提示、工具、控制规则），缺乏统一的强化学习优化接口；现有RL后训练框架仅支持单策略优化，无法处理用户定义的多智能体工作流、结构化交互、角色级信用分配和可配置的参数共享。

Method: 提出UnityMAS-O框架，将完整工作流作为优化单元，通过四个一等对象（逻辑智能体角色、图轨迹、用户定义奖励、智能体-模型映射）解耦逻辑智能体与物理模型参数，支持全共享、全分离和部分共享三种参数共享模式；基于Ray的星型拓扑运行时扩展verl，中央控制器执行工作流并记录轨迹，本地Worker组负责PPO式分布式更新。

Result: 在Natural Questions、HotpotQA和代码生成任务上，多智能体RL优化显著提升了手工指定工作流的性能，尤其对小模型和严格代码通过率指标提升明显。

Conclusion: UnityMAS-O可作为可复用基座，将多样化的LLM多智能体工作流转化为可训练的多智能体RL系统。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into interacting roles, but most remain manually orchestrated by prompts, tools, and control rules, while agents are rarely optimized through a unified reinforcement learning interface. Existing RL post-training frameworks mainly target single-policy optimization and lack abstractions for user-defined multi-agent workflows, structured interaction, role-specific credit assignment, and configurable parameter sharing.
  We present UnityMAS-O, a general RL optimization framework for LLM-based multi-agent systems. UnityMAS-O treats the complete workflow as the optimization unit, rather than a single response or policy trajectory. It represents workflows through four first-class objects: logical agent roles, graph trajectories, user-defined rewards, and agent--model mappings. This decouples logical agents from physical model parameters, supporting full sharing, full separation, and partial sharing, with rewards assigned at role, turn, and trajectory levels.
  UnityMAS-O extends verl with a Ray-based star-topology runtime. A central controller executes workflows, invokes tools, records structured trajectories, and assembles rewards; model-local worker groups handle rollout, buffering, advantage computation, and distributed PPO-style updates. Users can define agents, workflows, model mappings, and rewards without rewriting the optimization infrastructure.
  We instantiate UnityMAS-O on retrieval-augmented QA, iterative agentic search, and reflective code generation. Across Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and held-out code tasks, multi-agent RL improves manually specified workflows after optimization, with especially large gains for smaller models and strict code all-passed metrics. These results show that UnityMAS-O can serve as a reusable substrate for converting diverse LLM-based multi-agent workflows into trainable multi-agent RL systems.

</details>


### [208] [Completion vs Optimality: Policy Gradient in Long-Horizon Cumulative-Damage Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26657)
*Wolfgang Maass,Sabine Janzen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有累积损伤的长视界决策问题中策略梯度方法的两种失败模式（完成失败和最优性失败），提出分解方法，并通过砖匠和NBA球员两个独立校准环境验证了四项可复制预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长视界决策问题中，局部吸引的动作会累积导致全局不良后果，策略梯度方法在此类问题上存在两种正交的失败模式，需要系统性地识别和分解。

Method: 提出将策略梯度方法的失败分解为"完成"（到达终端视界）和"最优性"（匹配动态规划参考）两个正交维度；使用PPO配合线性软惩罚，在49步砖匠生涯和20赛季NBA大前锋生涯两个校准环境中评估预测。

Result: 单独赋予视界访问权会降低完成率；结合动作空间限制与视界访问虽能实现完成，但存在最优性差距（ΔM_final=0.271），源于损伤起始点的贪婪承诺；四项预测在两种环境中定性复制，视界不变性预测在四个视界中的三个成立，H=15处的异常与H*边界一致。

Conclusion: 成功识别并分解了累积损伤长视界问题中策略梯度的两种失败模式，并通过跨领域、跨视界的校准实验验证了理论预测的可复制性。

Abstract: Long-horizon decision problems with cumulative damage couple locally attractive actions to globally adverse outcomes. We identify two orthogonal failure modes for policy-gradient methods on this class and propose a decomposition that separates them: \emph{completion} (reaching the terminal horizon rather than exiting via an implicit terminal constraint) and \emph{optimality} (matching the dynamic-programming reference given completion). Under PPO with a linear soft penalty, granting horizon access alone reduces the completion rate: the penalty's equilibrium drives the dominant-activity share to zero, while action-space restriction combined with horizon access achieves completion but leaves an optimality gap ($ΔM_{\text{final}} = 0.271$) that we trace to first-phase greedy commitment at the damage origin. We derive four testable predictions and evaluate them in two separately calibrated environments that share the same abstract structure but differ in domain, horizon, activity set, and calibration data: a 49-step bricklayer career and a 20-season NBA power-forward career. All four predictions replicate qualitatively. The horizon-invariance prediction is met at three of four tested horizons, with the exception at $H = 15$ consistent with the $H^*$ boundary ($H^* \in [6, 14]$ under the NBA parameters).

</details>


### [209] [Mind the Tool Failures: Achieving Synergistic Tool Gains for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26691)
*Yunhui Gan,Tan Pan,Kaiyu Guo,Limei Han,Weimiao Yu,Guangnan Ye,Chen Jiang,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究医疗AI智能体在不完美工具场景下的工具使用问题，提出基于GRPO的强化学习框架，通过实例级工具选择、概率风险最小化和分歧感知协同学习，来纠正个体工具在困难实例上的失败，提升医疗智能体系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI智能体假设任务相关工具在其预期范围内是可靠的，但在实际临床中，即使相关的工具也可能在困难实例上失败，导致不安全的决策。个体工具存在实例依赖的失败模式，需要纠正这些被遗漏的失败实例。

Method: 1) 将工具使用建模为实例级选择问题；2) 提出基于GRPO的强化学习框架，包含概率风险最小化奖励和分歧感知协同学习；3) 采用熵引导的采样策略，增加高分歧实例的权重以学习实例特定的工具协同。

Result: 在两个任务和七个医疗基准上的实验表明，该方法在广泛的基线方法上实现了稳健且稳定的改进。

Conclusion: 分歧感知的工具协同使用对于构建可靠的医疗智能体系统至关重要，实例级异质性的缓解和工具协同的学习是提升系统可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Medical AI agents increasingly use external tools for diagnosis, treatment recommendation, and evidence retrieval, yet most existing approaches assume that task-appropriate tools are reliable within their intended scope. This assumption is fragile in real clinical settings, where even relevant tools may fail on challenging instances and lead to unsafe downstream decisions. To address this issue, we study medical tool use under imperfect-tool settings to correct failure instances missed by individual tools. Instance-dependent failure patterns create a gap between the best fixed single tool and an ideal instance-wise selector, which we refer to as the Single-Oracle risk gap. The core challenge is that conventional task-level tool selection cannot realize this gap, as it is inherently bounded by the performance of the best single tool. Motivated by this observation, we therefore account for instance-level heterogeneity and formulate tool use as an instance-level selection problem. Particularly, we propose a GRPO-based reinforcement learning framework with rewards for probabilistic risk minimization and disagreement-aware synergy learning, which promotes instance-level correction of erroneous tool consensus. Furthermore, an entropy-guided sampling strategy is adopted to upweight high-disagreement instances, which provide stronger signals for learning instance-specific tool synergy. These two components complement each other in mitigating instance-level heterogeneity and improving tool synergy. Experiments on two tasks and seven medical benchmarks show that our method consistently achieves robust and stable improvements over a broad range of baselines, highlighting the importance of synergy-aware tool use for reliable medical agentic systems.

</details>


### [210] [It's Not the Capability: Harness Sensitivity Is Non-Monotone Across LLM Agent Tiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26731)
*Yong-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在432次实验中评估6个模型（4个能力层级）在3种约束条件（宽松、平衡、严格）下的表现，本文否定了“模型能力越强所需结构引导越少”的单调假设，发现了约束复杂度悖论，并提出了基于模型类型的层级感知约束选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM agent部署中广泛存在的假设：更结构化的约束通用提升可靠性，且能力越强的模型需要越少的结构引导——即模型能力层级与最优约束复杂度之间存在单调反比关系。

Method: 在HEAT-24基准（24个合成任务，基于git工作区验证）上，跨越4个能力层级的6个模型，在3种约束条件（light、balanced、strict）下进行了432次受控实验。引入六标签失败分类法分析错误模式。

Result: 1) 前沿聊天模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash）中，约束越严格VTSR下降29-38个百分点，出现约束复杂度悖论；2) 前沿推理模型（Qwen3.5-122B）中，严格约束达到最高VTSR（91.7%）且延迟最低，与预测相反；3) 受限层级的2B模型（Gemma4:e2B）在所有约束下均达到91.7% VTSR，与强开放层级稳定性相当。格式违规主导高能力模型失败，错误文件主导低能力模型失败。

Conclusion: 约束敏感性在不同模型间呈现非单调关系，关键取决于模型类型（聊天vs推理），而非能力层级。需要层级感知的约束选择策略，并建议后续研究扩展每层级的模型数量以验证泛化性。

Abstract: A prevalent assumption in LLM agent deployment holds that more structured harnesses universally improve
  reliability, and that higher-capability models need proportionally less structural guidance -- together
  implying a monotone inverse relationship between model capability tier and optimal harness complexity. We
  test this hypothesis through a controlled 432-run experiment crossing six models across four capability
  tiers with three harness conditions (light, balanced, strict) on HEAT-24, a 24-task synthetic benchmark
  with git-based workspace verification. Our results refute the monotone inverse relationship on two
  fronts. First, for the frontier chat model evaluated (Gemini 2.5 Flash), increased harness verbosity
  lowers VTSR by 29-38 percentage points -- a harness-complexity paradox. Second, for the frontier
  reasoning model evaluated (Qwen3.5-122B, extended thinking enabled), strict harness achieves the highest
  VTSR (91.7%) and the lowest latency, the opposite of the prediction. Within the constrained tier, a 2B
  model (Gemma4:e2B) matches strong-open-tier stability at 91.7% across all harnesses. Because each tier is
  represented by a single model in this study, these results should be interpreted as model-specific
  observations; harness sensitivity appears non-monotone across the models evaluated, and depends
  critically on model type (chat vs. reasoning). We introduce a six-label failure taxonomy showing that
  format_violation dominates capable-model failures while wrong_file dominates low-capability failures, and
  we derive practical tier-aware harness selection guidelines.

</details>


### [211] [A Dataset of Robot-Patient and Doctor-Patient Medical Dialogues for Spoken Language Processing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26747)
*Heriberto Cuayahuitl,Grace Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MeDial-Speech语音数据集（111+小时，覆盖四种疾病），用于训练和评估能进行医疗咨询的Med-AI。通过句子选择基准测试（20选项）评估了三个LLM，发现Claude Sonnet 4准确率最高（71.1%-74.7%），但所有模型在概率预测上均过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在通用任务上表现出色，但应用于文本或语音医疗咨询仍是一个开放研究问题，缺乏专门针对医疗对话的语音数据集和评估基准。

Method: 构建MeDial-Speech数据集（从机器人与患者、医生与患者对话中收集，111+小时，覆盖路易体痴呆、心力衰竭、肩痛、心绞痛四种疾病）；提出基于句子选择（20选项）的对话基准，评估GPT-5 mini、DeepSeek-V3和Claude Sonnet 4三个LLM。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4在句子选择任务中表现最佳（人工转录71.1%，自动转录74.7%）；所有LLM在概率预测上均表现出高度过度自信，无论选择是否正确。

Conclusion: MeDial-Speech数据集可用于训练和评估医疗咨询AI；当前LLM在医疗对话任务中虽有一定能力，但过度自信问题需要关注；数据集免费提供非商业用途。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have brought huge improvements to Artificial Intelligence (AI), which can be applied to general-purpose tasks. However, their application to textual or spoken medical consultations is still an open research problem. This paper proposes MeDial-Speech, a novel speech dataset for training and evaluating Med-AIs that can carry out consultations with patients. It was collected in realistic environments from robot-patient and doctor-patient dialogues, contains 111+ hours of speech data (without data augmentation), and covers four health conditions: Lewy body dementia, heart failure, shoulder pain, and angina. In addition, we propose a dialogue benchmark via sentence selection (with 20 options) to evaluate three state-of-the-art LLMs: GPT-5 mini, DeepSeek-V3, and Claude Sonnet 4. Experimental results reveal that Claude Sonnet 4 is the best in sentence selection, with 71.1% accuracy using manual transcriptions and 74.7% using automatic transcriptions, and that all LLMs are highly overconfident in their probabilistic predictions, regardless of selecting correct or incorrect sentences in medical dialogues. This dataset is free of charge for non-commercial purposes at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/hcuayahu/MeDial-Speech

</details>


### [212] [Beyond a Single Direction: Chain-of-Thought Disrupts Simple Steering of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26772)
*Kia-Jüng Yang,Dominik Meier,Jiachen Zhao,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型推理模型（LRM）的拒绝机制不仅依赖残差流激活，还依赖思维链（CoT）的显式推理过程。CoT本身可以独立携带并重构顺从信号，使得仅针对激活层的干预效果有限，但CoT也暴露出新的攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型推理模型（LRM）与传统指令微调LLM在拒绝机制上的差异，特别是思维链（CoT）对拒绝行为的影响，以及这种联合编码机制对安全控制的潜在挑战。

Method: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B上进行激活操控实验，分别测试三种条件：固定CoT下进行激活操控、移除CoT后进行激活操控、以及激活操控下重新生成CoT的两阶段干预。同时测试仅使用重新生成的CoT（移除激活操控）的效果。

Result: 固定CoT时激活操控仅能反转39%的拒绝；移除CoT后提升至70%；两阶段干预下拒绝反转率达94%；而仅保留重新生成的CoT（无激活操控）仍能维持48%的效果。

Conclusion: LRM中的拒绝机制是残差流激活与CoT联合编码的，CoT可以独立携带并重构顺从信号。这种双重机制使LRM对单纯激活层干预具有更强鲁棒性，但也暴露了CoT作为新的攻击面。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. Unlike instruction-tuned LLMs, where refusal is mediated by a single directional subspace, refusal in large reasoning models (LRMs) additionally depends on the CoT. In DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, activation steering reverses refusal in only 39% of cases when the CoT is kept fixed, but removing the CoT entirely increases this to 70%, indicating that the CoT actively reinforces refusal. In a two-stage intervention where the model regenerates its CoT under activation steering, refusal is reversed in 94% of cases, while the resulting CoT alone retains 48% of this effect even after steering is removed. This suggests that the CoT can carry and reconstruct the compliance signal independently. These findings indicate that refusal in LRMs is jointly encoded in residual stream activations and CoT. This joint activation makes LRM more robust against activation-level interventions alone, but exposes CoT to a possible alternative surface attack.

</details>


### [213] [The Attribution Blind Spot: Detecting When Language Models Rely on Memory Rather Than Retrieved Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26778)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Bo Yang,Chen Ye,Gaolei Li,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现检索增强生成(RAG)中存在"归因盲点"：当检索文档与模型预训练数据重叠时，模型可能完全依靠参数记忆生成与上下文一致的输出，而现有输出级方法无法区分。作者提出计算现实监控(CRM)方法，通过比较有无上下文时的内部表征差异来检测预训练暴露的痕迹，在三个模型家族的九个变体上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统假设上下文一致的输出即受上下文约束，但该假设在检索文档与预训练数据重叠时失效——模型可完全从参数记忆生成忠实文本，两种路径产生不可区分的输出。现有输出级监控无法发现这一盲点，亟需可验证上下文是否真正主导生成的方法。

Method: CRM借鉴认知科学中的现实监控框架，通过比较有/无上下文条件下模型内部表征的差异（成员条件性表征发散），检测预训练暴露留下的可测量内部轨迹特征。该方法不认证单个生成的具体来源，而是检测预训练暴露是否留下可测量的内部表征信号。

Result: 在三个模型家族（九个变体）上，表征发散集中在架构相关的特定层模式中；块级噪声干预提供了收敛支持证据；该方法跨任务和数据集泛化，但在领域混淆的基准上失效。

Conclusion: 归因盲点可测量且部分可解：内部表征携带着输出级不可见的诊断信号，为构建具备证据来源内部感知能力的系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation promises to ground language model outputs in external evidence, yet the field has no reliable way to verify whether retrieved context actually governs generation -- a prerequisite for any high-stakes deployment. The standard assumption, that context-consistent output implies context-governed output, breaks when the retrieved document overlaps with the model's pretraining data: the model can produce faithful-looking text entirely from parametric memory, and both pathways yield indistinguishable output. We name this failure the attribution blind spot and introduce Computational Reality Monitoring (CRM) to address it. CRM operationalizes a principle adapted from cognitive science's reality monitoring framework: comparing internal representations with and without context reveals membership-conditioned representational divergence that output-level monitors systematically miss. CRM does not certify which source an individual generation used; it detects whether pretraining exposure leaves a measurable internal trajectory signature, establishing a necessary substrate for source attribution. Across nine model variants spanning three families, this divergence concentrates in architecture-specific layer patterns, receives converging support from block-level noise intervention, and generalizes across tasks and datasets while collapsing on domain-confounded benchmarks. The attribution blind spot is measurable and partially addressable: internal representations carry a diagnostic signal invisible at the output level, establishing a foundation for systems whose internal awareness of evidence provenance governs their external behavior.

</details>


### [214] [LiveK12Bench: Have Large Multimodal Models Truly Conquered High School-level Examinations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26781)
*Xiaohan Wang,Mingze Yin,Yilin Zhao,Gang Liu,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LiveK12Bench，一个动态、多学科的K-12教育基准测试，包含2000+道来自最新真实考试的最新题目，通过自动化流水线持续更新以防止数据泄露，并引入"模拟考试"评估方案来检验大语言模型在真实考试场景下的端到端推理能力。实验表明，在考试真实约束下，GPT-5的得分从79降至53（满分100），揭示了现有模型在复杂视觉布局等方面的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多为静态数据集，容易受到数据污染，且局限于有限的模态、学科和评估标准，无法真实反映LMMs在现实考试环境中的复杂表现，因此需要构建一个动态、全面、多学科的评估基准。

Method: 1) 构建自动化流水线持续获取和解析最新真实考试试卷，以缓解数据泄露问题；2) 提出"模拟考试"评估方案，评估模型在端到端考试中自主完成准确和高效推理路径的能力。

Result: 在12个LMMs上的实验表明，在考试真实约束下，GPT-5的得分从79降至53（满分100），揭示了模型在复杂视觉布局等方面的关键脆弱性。

Conclusion: 当前先进LMMs在理想化推理能力与真实教育场景准备之间存在显著差距，模型对复杂视觉布局的敏感度等问题亟待解决。

Abstract: Advanced Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in K-12 reasoning tasks, exhibiting great promise as intelligent tutors. Realizing this potential requires models to navigate real-world examinations effectively, yet most existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of authentic testing environments. Specifically, most datasets are static, prone to data contamination, and are often confined to restricted modalities, disciplines, and evaluation criteria. To address these issues, we introduce LiveK12Bench, a dynamic, holistic, multi-disciplinary benchmark designed to evaluate the reasoning abilities of LMMs in realistic examination scenarios. LiveK12Bench comprises 2K+ verified questions spanning Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, and Biology, sourced from the latest real-world exam papers and designed to grow over time. Our framework features several core innovations: 1) featuring an automated pipeline that continuously ingests and parses the latest examination papers to mitigate data leakage; and 2) proposing a novel `Mock Exam' evaluation scheme, which assesses the ability to complete end-to-end exams autonomously with accurate and efficient reasoning paths. Extensive experiments on 12 LMMs reveal that advanced models suffer substantial performance degradation under exam-realistic constraints: GPT-5's score drops from 79 to 53 (out of 100) when process rigor and efficiency are jointly evaluated. Our findings expose critical vulnerabilities, such as sensitivity to complex visual layouts, highlighting the gap between idealized reasoning capabilities and true educational readiness. Both code and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [215] [Composition Collapse: Stable Factual Knowledge Does Not Imply Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26789)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Jie Chen,Hongzhi Wang,Xuyang Teng,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现后训练方法在多跳推理评估中存在"合成崩溃"现象：在原子知识掌握程度统计无差异的情况下，合成行为差异可达40个百分点以上。作者提出"双门控协议"，将传统聚合组合性差距重新定义为基于稳定原子访问的条件残留组合失败，并将后训练收益分解为原子稳定性、残留组合和关键深度三个独立通道。实验表明，后训练目标实际影响合成能力的方向被聚合指标掩盖，并建议未来多跳推理提升的声明应附带原子门控组合指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前后训练评估中聚合基准分数的误导性——它们将多跳推理视为单一能力，忽略了原子知识稳定但组合失败（即"合成崩溃"）的系统性问题，并提出更精确的分解评估方法。

Method: 提出双门控协议（double-gate protocol），将评估目标从聚合的组合性差距转变为条件于稳定原子访问的残留组合失败；将后训练收益分解为原子稳定性、残留组合和关键深度三个独立通道；在深度2-11的时间事实链基准上对四种后训练方法进行实验，并使用诊断探针区分永久性无法合成与生成时计算约束导致的失败。

Result: 在原子知识统计无差异的后训练方法间，合成行为差距超过40个百分点；后训练目标对组合能力的影响方向被聚合指标掩盖；诊断探针显示大量测量到的合成失败源于生成时的计算约束而非永久性能力缺失。

Conclusion: 现有聚合指标会掩盖后训练在多跳推理上的真实影响，建议未来多跳推理改进的声明应附带原子门控组合指标；合成失败中相当比例可通过改善生成时计算约束缓解。

Abstract: Post-training is routinely evaluated through aggregate benchmark scores that treat multi-hop reasoning as a single capability -- as if a model that answers more questions correctly must be better at assembling facts. We show that this assumption can be misleading: recipes with statistically indistinguishable atomic knowledge produce composition behaviour separated by over 40 percentage points, a phenomenon we call composition collapse: the systematic failure to assemble stably-known facts into chains, invisible to aggregate metrics. We introduce a double-gate protocol that changes the estimand from an aggregate compositionality gap to residual composition failure conditioned on stable atomic access, decomposing post-training gains into three independent channels: atomic stability, residual composition, and critical depth. On a benchmark of temporal factual chains spanning depths 2--11 across four post-training recipes, this decomposition reveals that post-training objectives shift composition capability in directions that aggregate metrics mask, and suggests that claims about multi-hop reasoning improvement should be accompanied by atomic-gate-controlled composition metrics. Diagnostic probes further show that a substantial share of measured composition failure reflects generation-time computation constraints rather than permanent inability to compose.

</details>


### [216] [What Makes Chain-of-Thought Work at Probe Time? Local Co-occurrence Rather Than Global Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26795)
*Xiang Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过在推理时（probe-time）固定思维链（CoT）文本，发现CoT提升模型准确率主要来自两个互补来源：全局词序打乱后的词汇激活效应，以及短距离（2-3个token）共现关系，而非句子级的逻辑推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注CoT生成阶段的行为，但对推理阶段（probe-time）给定固定解释文本后，文本中哪些具体属性驱动了性能提升尚不明确。

Method: 固定CoT解释文本，通过全局词序打乱、保留不同长度连续窗口等操作，系统性地分离词汇激活效应与结构信息贡献，并在多种模型族、参数规模和数据集上进行泛化验证。

Result: 全局词序打乱的CoT显著优于无CoT基线；保留2-3个token的连续窗口即可恢复大部分结构化文本带来的增益；显式答案声明、完整语法实现等并非主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 推理时CoT的增益主要来自词汇激活和短距离token共现（局部共现激活，LCA），而非句子级的逻辑推导。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting reliably improves language-model accuracy, but which properties of a rationale text drive the improvement is poorly understood. Prior work has largely studied generation-time behavior. We instead ask a probe-time question: given a fixed rationale in context, what in that text changes the answer? We identify two complementary sources of the gain. First, even a globally word-shuffled rationale substantially outperforms the no-rationale baseline, indicating a strong lexical activation effect. More importantly, the additional gain from structured text appears to arise less from sentence-level logical ordering and more from short-range token adjacency. Preserving contiguous windows of just $n^\star{=}2$--$3$ tokens recovers most of the remaining gain toward full CoT performance. Supporting experiments rule out copying of explicit answer declarations or answer values, as well as full grammatical realization, as primary drivers. Further generalization experiments show that the qualitative pattern remains stable across multiple model families, parameter scales, and datasets. These results support a local co-occurrence activation (LCA) account of probe-time CoT, in which the observed gains appear to arise primarily from lexical activation and short-range token co-occurrence rather than sentence-level logical derivation.

</details>


### [217] [Multi-Stakeholder LLM Alignment: Decomposing Estimation from Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26878)
*Lulu Zheng,Wenjin Yang,Xiangwen Zhang,Rong Yin,Yulan Hu,Zheng Pan,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多利益相关方任务中LLM整体评估存在的权重噪声问题，提出DecompR方法，通过反事实校准固定权重并独立估计角色效用，消除了候选依赖的权重漂移并降低了估计噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多利益相关方任务需要单个输出同时满足存在冲突偏好的多个用户，而整体式LLM评估器将效用估计与效用聚合混为一谈，产生不稳定的隐式权重，导致评分偏移。

Method: 提出DecompR方法：从查询结构固定反事实校准的权重（在候选评分之前），同时独立估计每个角色的效用，从而消除候选依赖的权重漂移并降低估计噪声。

Result: 从理论和实验上证明，聚合特定的"权重噪声"会在利益相关方满意度分散时产生较大的评分偏移，且这种偏移随利益相关方数量增加而增大；DecompR有效解决了该问题。

Conclusion: DecompR通过分解效用估计与聚合过程，解决了整体式LLM评估器在多利益相关方任务中权重不稳定的核心缺陷。

Abstract: Multi-stakeholder tasks require one output to satisfy users with conflicting preferences. Holistic LLM judges conflate utility estimation and utility aggregation, yielding unstable implicit weights. We show empirically and theoretically that this aggregation-specific \emph{weighting noise} can create large score shifts when stakeholder satisfaction is dispersed; in our experiments, these weight-induced shifts also increase with stakeholder count. We propose \textsc{DecompR}: counterfactual-calibrated weights are fixed from query structure before candidate scoring, while per-role utilities are estimated independently, removing candidate-dependent weight drift and reducing estimation noise.

</details>


### [218] [On the Detection of Commutative Factors in Factor Graphs: Necessary and Sufficient Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26908)
*Malte Luttermann,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有检测因子图中交换因子的SOTA算法存在理论基础缺陷（将必要条件误当作充分条件），通过修正定理并提出两种修正算法来保证正确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA算法依赖的核心定理被错误地视为交换因子识别的充分条件，实际上仅为必要条件，导致算法可能输出错误结果。需要修正理论基础并修复算法缺陷。

Method: 1）重新证明修正版定理，明确其为交换因子识别的必要条件；2）提出修正版SOTA算法，保持原有效率同时确保正确性；3）引入一种具有更紧最坏情况界的新算法作为补充。

Result: 修正后的算法在理论上保证了正确性，修正版算法保持了原有计算效率，补充算法则提供了更紧的最坏情况复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 通过修正理论基础和算法设计，解决了提升概率推理中交换因子检测的正确性问题，为 lifted 推理的可信执行提供了保障。

Abstract: Exploiting the indistinguishability of objects in a probabilistic graphical model such as a factor graph is key to lifted probabilistic inference algorithms and allows for tractable probabilistic inference problems with respect to domain sizes. A central building block for the exploitation of indistinguishable objects in factor graphs is the identification of commutative factors, i.e., factors whose output values are invariant under permutations of input values assigned to a subset of their arguments. In this paper, we revisit the theoretical foundations underlying the state-of-the-art algorithm to detect commutative factors. Specifically, we show that in its current form, the state-of-the-art algorithm relies on a central theorem that is mistakenly regarded as a sufficient condition to identify commutative factors, while it actually only implies necessary condition. Consequently, the state of the art might, as we show in this paper, deliver incorrect results. To fix the flaws currently present in the state of the art, we prove a slightly modified version of the aforementioned theorem, which serves as a necessary condition to identify commutative factors. Moreover, we present a corrected version of the state-of-the-art algorithm, which keeps its efficiency while ensuring correctness and introduce a complementary algorithm with tighter worst-case bounds.

</details>


### [219] [TADDLE: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Detecting Deficient LLM-Generated Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26911)
*Hanqi Duan,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TADDLE，一个基于工具增强的智能体系统，用于检测LLM生成的同行评审中的缺陷，并构建了首个专家标注的多标签基准数据集（1800条评审，含六类缺陷标签），通过四项专用分析工具和两阶段半监督学习实现缺陷检测与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作要么仅分类评审是否为LLM生成而不评判质量，要么使用为人类评审设计的特征进行质量评分，缺乏在单个缺陷类型层面检测LLM生成评审缺陷的系统。

Method: 将缺陷检测分解为四个专用分析工具（Verify、Correct、Complete、Transform），由智能体编排；通过两阶段半监督学习将工具输出整合为二元检测和多标签分类结果。

Result: 在二元检测和多标签分类任务上均表现优异。

Conclusion: TADDLE能有效检测LLM生成同行评审中的各类缺陷，填补了该领域的空白，并公开了基准数据集和代码。

Abstract: LLM-generated peer reviews are increasingly common at major venues, yet their deficiencies are hard to detect because they are uniformly fluent and well-structured. Existing work either classifies authorship without judging quality, or scores quality with features designed for human-written reviews; no prior system detects deficiencies in LLM-generated reviews at the level of individual defect types. To bridge the gap, we introduce TADDLE, a Tool-Augmented Agent for Detecting Deficient LLM-Generated Peer Reviews, together with the first expert-annotated benchmark for this task. Our benchmark comprises 1,800 reviews on 50 ICLR 2025 papers, multi-label-annotated by 18 domain experts against a taxonomy of six defect categories (plus a non-deficient label). TADDLE decomposes detection into four specialized analysis tools -- Verify, Correct, Complete, and Transform -- orchestrated by an agent; an integrator synthesizes their outputs into binary and multi-label classifications via two-stage semi-supervised learning. Extensive experiments show that TADDLE performs strongly on both binary detection and the multi-label classification task. We release the benchmark and code at https://github.com/AquariusAQ/TADDLE.

</details>


### [220] [From Norms to Indicators (N2I-RAG): An Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework for Legal Indicator Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26926)
*Youssef Al Mouatamid,Marie Bonnin,Jihad Zahir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了N2I-RAG框架，一种基于智能体检索增强生成的方法，用于从规范性文本中自动、透明、可追溯地计算法律指标。该框架在法国海洋环境法语料库上取得了优于基线的结果，并具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律指标计算面临法律语言复杂性、文本规模大、解释性强以及文档质量参差不齐等挑战，现有NLP和生成模型存在高幻觉风险，且缺乏可解释性和证据基础，难以满足可靠指标计算的需求。

Method: 提出N2I-RAG框架，集成自适应检索、基于LLM的智能体和验证机制，构建模块化流水线。每个组件负责过滤、检索和评估证据，生成可追溯至具体法律条文的二元法律结果，并要求对中间决策和最终指标分配提供显式解释。

Result: 在自建的法国海洋环境法语料库（含扫描和数字源）上，与多个语言模型家族的对比实验表明，N2I-RAG始终优于基线系统，并在两种不同禁令测试中表现良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 智能体检索增强生成能够弥合开放式法律语言与标准化指标计算之间的鸿沟，为构建透明、可扩展的法律观测站提供了基础。

Abstract: Computing legal indicators from normative texts is a key task in legal monitoring and policy evaluation, but presents significant challenges due to the complexity, scale, and interpretive nature of legal language, as well as the variability in available document quality. Existing natural language processing techniques and generative models can assist in legal analysis, but often suffer from high risk of hallucinations and lack the interpretability and evidence grounding required for reliable indicator computation. This paper presents N2I-RAG (From Norms to Indicators), an agentic retrieval-augmented generation framework designed to automate the computation of legal indicators in a transparent and traceable way. We integrate adaptive retrieval, llm-based agents, and validation mechanisms in a modular pipeline, where each component performs a defined role in filtering, retrieving, and assessing evidence, and in producing binary legal outcomes linked to identifiable legal provisions. The framework emphasizes traceability by requiring explicit explanations of intermediate decisions and final indicator assignments. We evaluate N2I-RAG using an in-house constructed French marine environmental law corpus that includes both scanned and digital sources. Comparative experiments with multiple language model families demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms baseline systems, and generalizes well when tested on 2 different bans. The results indicate that agentic retrieval-augmented generation can bridge open-text legal language and standardized indicator computation, offering a foundation for transparent and scalable legal observatories.

</details>


### [221] [Neuro-Symbolic Verification of LLM Outputs for Data-Sensitive Domains (extended preprint)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26942)
*Paul Sigloch,Christoph Benzmüller*

Main category: cs.AI
